Chapter 303: Supernatural City

“Well, Mr. Ji, you didn’t mention remuneration before…”

The Old Bull asked awkwardly.

“It’s true that I didn’t mention it, but you didn’t ask either.”

Ji Yuan teased with a smile, watching the Old Bull’s face change into a constipated expression.

“Yes, but this Old Bull doesn’t have anything to offer as payment… How about I kowtow to you, sir?”

Ji Yuan shook his head and stopped teasing him.

“Sit down and disperse the spiritual energy on the back of your neck. This hair is rooted in your marrow and essence. It will take some effort to remove it. Don’t move, even if you feel something, otherwise, if there is any deviation while guiding the True Fire energy, you will suffer.”

Niu Batian was overjoyed at Ji Yuan’s words. He agreed and sat down cross-legged with his back facing Ji Yuan, lowering his head and pulling down his collar.

Looking at the campfire in front of him, Niu Batian began imagining what Mr. Ji’s True Fire of Samadhi might look like.

“Prepare yourself, don’t move rashly, the True Fire of Samadhi is no joke!”

Ji Yuan’s words became much more serious, and the Old Bull hurriedly collected himself. Yan Fei, who was sitting on the opposite side of the fire, originally resting against a tree trunk with his sword in his arms, now sat up straight and observed from the side.

Ji Yuan gazed at the old bull’s rough skin and the tuft of brown hair at the nape of his neck, and slowly opened his supernatural eyes wide.

Compared to the blurriness of normal sights, Ji Yuan’s vision was unusually clear when it came to special auras, energies, and divine light.

The Old Bull’s demonic aura and the demonic aura of the hair possessed two completely different attributes. Following the collision of these contradictory auras, the line of sight of Ji Yuan’s supernatural eyes seemed to extend down into the depths of the bull’s spirit. He could see the source of that evil energy constantly absorbing the Old Bull’s essence and spiritual energy, attempting to continuously extend, but there was a thin membrane of spiritual light blocking it.

“It’s indeed formidable.”

Ji Yuan declared. He summoned the spiritual energy in his body to open the golden bridge in his inner world, sending forth a wisp of True Fire energy. He opened his mouth and a stream of reddish-gray energy slowly escaped, approaching the back of Niu Batian’s neck.

As the reddish-gray energy approached, the bull demon clutched at his trousers. Unable to control his force, he tore them apart as he clamped down on his own leg.

While there should not have been any fluctuations in temperature behind him, his spiritual perception gave him the illusion that there was a raging sea of ​​fire behind him, along with a mental sensation like the piercing of endless needles.

Instinctual warnings raced though his mind.

‘Danger! Very dangerous! Fatal danger! I must dodge! ‘

Even so, Niu Batian resisted the urge to flee immediately and remained motionless. His clenched teeth made a cracking sound, and cold sweat dripped from his body. The bonfire in front of him was a mere drop in the ocean compared to the endless sea of flames behind him.

“Whoosh…”

The faintest sound of breathing was magnified countless times in this tense moment. Niu Batian’s pupils constricted violently as he faced the bonfire. The monstrous, raging sea of fire was already close by.

A wisp of reddish-gray energy flew under Ji Yuan’s fine control directly to the back of Niu Batian’s exposed neck, and touched the tuft of brown hair.

However, this time, the reddish-gray energy did not directly ignite the hair; instead, it flowed along the hair from the tip, penetrating the Old Bull’s skin, and continued to flow downward. At this moment, the True Fire energy was as thin as a gossamer strand.

This process was an extreme test of Ji Yuan’s mental and spiritual strength, as well as his control, and, of course, Niu Batian’s endurance.

“Hold on and don’t move! The True Fire has already entered your body. If you wish to eradicate this curse, you must wait for it to take root. If you directly let it burn, your soul will also be burned. Concentrate your mind and calm yourself. Don’t visualize the True Fire behind you, otherwise your state of mind will be unable to bear it!”

Ji Yuan reminded him again, then threw the piece of yellow paper from his sleeve and stopped distracting himself, trying his best to control the raging True Fire. Niu Batian immediately forced himself into stillness, as if he had just woken up from a dream.

Before the yellow paper hit the ground, a faint yellow light, like glowing powder, spread out in the air, and it immediately transformed into a very muscular Golden-Armored Warrior.

The golden armored warrior faced Ji Yuan and bowed respectfully.

“Your Honor!”

But Ji Yuan had no time to pay attention to him, so the Golden-Armored Warrior finished his bow, his gaze only lingering on Yan Fei for a moment, then he stood quietly by the campfire and didn’t move.

Yan Fei watched nervously from the side. Although he couldn’t figure out what was going on, he knew that this was a critical moment. He had heard Niu Batian complain more than once about this evil technique on the back of the neck, saying that it was a vicious technique that damaged his cultivation foundation.

However, with the appearance of this Golden-Armored Warrior, it was clearly not his place to help protect the two cultivators.

Ordinary people could not concentrate for that long of a time, not even Yan Fei with his excellent martial arts skills. After staring at the scene on the opposite side of the campfire for about half an hour, he finally couldn’t bear it anymore.

He rubbed his eyes and looked at the Golden-Armored Warrior. This burly general didn’t move a muscle, and he didn’t even blink his eyes.

“Haah… Please, Warrior, take care of Mr. Ji and Senior Brother Niu. I’ll be back soon.”

When he was drunk, he had been calling him “Brother Niu,” but this honorific was too intimate, and Yan Fei wasn’t used to it, so he had just been using “Senior Brother Niu” over this period of time.1

Yan leapt to the campfire and tossed another large piece of wood into it, then stood up from where he was kneeling and prepared to go out to relieve himself.

The night wind blew by the edge of the woods. Even in the fourth lunar month, it was still chilly. After he had walked a few steps away, Yan Fei turned his head to look in the direction of the campfire.

‘It would be better to go further away for convenience.’

With this thought, Yan Fei took a deep breath and used his movement techniques to step like the wind, quickly traveling towards the depths of the woods.

It was the springtime season of the world’s rebirth, and this forest was no longer silent at night. Various insects and frogs were chirping constantly, so there was probably a source of water nearby.

Now that the flames of the campfire were almost out of sight, Yan Fei stopped and unbuttoned his pants. No matter how far away he was, this distance should not affect Mr. Ji.

The line of water fell onto the dead branches and leaves on the ground, and Yan Fei’s expression relaxed. However, during this process, he opened his eyes and looked around, only to find that he had passed through the densest belt of the forest. Now, looking further forward, he could actually see through the forest to the other side.

“Is there a fire?”

A faint glow could be seen in the distance.

There shouldn’t be any towns or villages nearby, so these flames could only be from another group of people.

After he had finished relieving himself, Yan Fei fastened his belt and glanced back at the direction of the campfire, where Ji Yuan and Niu Batian currently were. He then looked forwards into the distance, gathered his strength, and moved onwards. It took him but a moment to reach the edge of the woods on the other side.

Yan Fei then found a taller tree, leaped up, pushed off a branch, and landed directly at the crown, gazing out into the distance.

“There really is light, and there’s a lot of it…”

From his perch, Yan Fei realized that this distant glow wasn’t just a simple fire. Although it was far off, it wasn’t a single point. This wasn’t something that could be explained away as a campfire; it looked more like a wildfire.

Yan Fei looked up at the sky. There wasn’t even a single cloud or wisp of smoke to be seen in the dim moonlight.

‘The direction of the wind is towards me. If it were a wildfire, there would be smoke. Could it be… the lights of the town? But that’s impossible, there isn’t so much as a village within a hundred li of this place, there couldn’t possibly be a town.’

After thinking for a while, Yan Fei shook his head. He figured that it was better to have fewer worries than more of them, so he prepared to jump off the branch. However, before he jumped, he suddenly thought he saw a carriage rolling through the wilderness a little further away. He took a closer look: it was indeed a horse-drawn carriage.

The carriage was at least one li away from the edge of the woods where Yan Fei was, and it was driving from east to west. It seemed to be headed in the direction of the fire Yan Fei had spotted. He frowned and looked for a while, before finally jumping off the branch and turning around to head back.

At this moment, at the campfire at the other end of the forest, Ji Yuan had controlled the True Fire energy and touched the root of the hair. The True Fire energy was like a tiny tornado, engulfing it from the outside in. Then, Ji Yuan’s mind moved.

Whoosh… All the brown hair and demonic spiritual energy were incinerated in an instant.

“Hiss…”

Niu Batian made a sound of pain, or more likely of fear.

“Okay, it’s resolved. Without a detailed understanding of this technique, we can only burn it out crudely like this.”

“Haah…haah…Just now, this Old Bull was really frightened. But it’s finally done.”

Niu Batian wiped the sweat off his face, and then reached out to touch the back of his neck. Aside from a burnt patch of skin about the size of a fingernail, there was no other pain. More importantly, he felt relieved, and his instincts no longer felt any sense of threat.

“Hahahahaha, hahahahaha… It’s really gone, that’s amazing! Hahahahaha… When I fully recover, I won’t be afraid if I meet that stinky bitch again!”

“With your hobby, you might get tricked again.”

Ji Yuan laughed.

“Hey, hey, how could that be? Ever failure is a lesson. From now on, this Old Bull will be more cautious! By the way, where is Brother Yan?”

The Old Bull made a promise, but didn’t see Yan Fei.

“He must have gone out to relieve himself. He should be back soon.”

Sure enough, as Ji Yuan said, Yan Fei returned to the campfire only moments later. He saw that Ji Yuan had already pulled out a book again and Niu Batian was dozing off, each busy with their own things.

Ji Yuan didn’t bother to look up at the sound of Yan Fei’s footsteps, but Niu Batian leapt to his feet. He needed to share his excitement with others.

“Ah, Brother Yan, you’re back. Haha, your Brother Niu is healed again. Now that this evil technique has been removed, there’s nothing stopping my recovery. That bitch can’t defeat me any more. Look, look, the hair is gone!”

The Old Bull turned around and showed Yan Fei the back of his neck. Yan Fei simply congratulated him a few times, then relayed what he had seen.

As Ji Yuan listened, he put down the book. Even the Old Bull fell silent.

“You said there might be a town here?”

“Yes. I saw a horse-drawn carriage heading in that direction with my own eyes, and the firelight is arranged in a way that would indicate a town. Mr. Ji, shall we go and have a look?”

Niu Batian glanced at Ji Yuan and said.

“Mr. Ji, something isn’t right here. There shouldn’t be any towns here. After all, the gathered vital energy of people gathered in towns is so intense that you can’t help but see it.”

Ji Yuan put away the book in his hand, stood up, and looked in the direction Yan Fei had indicated. Indeed, he did not see any signs of a gathering of people.

“Let’s go and take a look. You two, follow me.”

Before he had even finished speaking, Ji Yuan had already set off. His body seemed to be moving slowly, as if he were on a stroll, but he had traveled far in only a short period of time.

“Brother Yan, let’s go too!”

The Old Bull grabbed Yan Fei’s arm. A faint yellow halo appeared under his feet, and he hurriedly chased after him.

In less than a quarter of an hour, the three of them had arrived on a sloping hill, looking far off to the west.

“It really is a town! Who lives here? Why haven’t I heard of it?”

Yan Fei asked in disbelief, but Niu Batian sneered.

“Hmph, Brother Yan, you’re wrong. This town is real, but it’s not for humans!”

T/n: things always happen when a character goes to pee in the middle of the woods

哥 (gē) is more informal than 兄 (xiōng), though they both translate to “elder brother.” I’ve chosen “Brother” for the informal one and “Elder Brother” for the more formal translation.

Chapter 304: Switching the World From Day to Night

[T/n: note on chapter title]1

Hearing this, Yan Fei’s heart immediately trembled. The idea of a “city of the dead” naturally emerged in his mind. He subconsciously looked at Ji Yuan, and saw that he looked to be deep in thought.

From where the three of them were, looking into the distance, they could see a walled city. From the city gate to everywhere else in the city, everything was brightly lit, with large lanterns hanging.

In ordinary cities, this kind of sight could only appear during large-scale festivals like the Lantern Festival.2

“Mr. Ji, what do you think?”

Niu Batian asked Ji Yuan. Of the three of them, Ji Yuan was the one who could really make the decision; this idea had been subtly ingrained in him over the past twenty days.

Ji Yuan didn’t speak immediately. He looked over at the city in the distance, then looked above the city. There was a thin layer of clouds gathering, blocking the light of the stars and the moon in the sky, but most other areas of the sky were clear tonight.

“The Yin Qi is forming clouds, but there is no obvious external presence. It seems that it is not a spontaneous surge of Yin Qi, but rather an intentional conversion. There must be a skilled ghost cultivator in the city.”

Having spoken, Ji Yuan thought for a moment more.

“It’s brightly lit over there, so it shouldn’t be any ordinary occasion. Let’s wait and see. We won’t join in on the fun if it’s not necessary.”

“Mhm!”

Yan Fei responded. After all, the warrior was still human no matter his strength. He had no desire to enter a city of the dead, where he imagined thousands of ghosts would congregate.

Niu Batian, on the other hand, was a little curious. Now that Ji Yuan had decreed they wouldn’t continue, he felt very restless inside.

“Hey, Mr. Ji, look: this Old Bull has never been to an orthodox City of the Dead in the underworld either. It looks quite lively over there. Why don’t we go for a stroll? Brother Yan probably wants to visit it too, right?”

Yan Fei quickly shook his head, ignoring Niu Batian’s urging looks.

“Elder Brother Niu, I’m a mortal, how could I venture into a ghost town? I would definitely not even entertain such a thought!”

Ji Yuan shook his head, and sat down cross-legged on the hill.

“Let’s wait until dawn, then we can investigate what this city’s background is. It looks so lively; if we rush in now, we may offend someone.”

With Ji Yuan’s words, Niu Batian couldn’t say anything more, so he could only sigh regretfully.

In the night, Ji Yuan rested as he sat with his legs crossed, while Niu Batian and Yan Fei kept glancing at the distant city. They had not seen a single person entering or leaving the city ever since they had arrived here. Yan Fei’s glimpse of the horse-drawn carriage earlier might have been a special case.

“Hey, hey, Brother Yan, someone– ah, no, a ghost– passed by!”

Niu Batian suddenly cried out in excitement. This was the first time since he had cultivated his demon body that he had been so excited at the sight of a ghost.

“Huh? Where?”

Yan Fei opened his eyes wide and but couldn’t see it. Clearly, he wasn’t able to see ghosts. The Old Bull, realizing this, hurriedly approached him.

“Come on, let me help you!”

With that, Niu Batian stretched out his hand and flicked Yan Fei’s forehead with his index finger.

“Thunk…” There was a soft sound, and Yan Fei suddenly felt a little dizzy. The moment his head was hit, his eyes went black and there seemed to be a flash of white light.

When it had passed, he looked down, and suddenly saw that there were some people who looked to be soldiers riding horses far away from the city. Behind them was a line of figures bound by ropes. Some were floating, while others were walking. Some had long necks, some were missing arms or legs.

“Hiss… this, these…”

“Ah, they are all wandering ghosts. Many people died outside for unknown reasons and had no relatives or friends to take care of their funerary rites. In many places, the gods are fading, and the dead are very likely to become wandering ghosts. It’s like this everywhere in Zuyue.”

It was the first time Yan Fei had heard such a statement. He felt horrified.

“Then don’t these ghosts harm people?”

Ji Yuan opened his eyes at this moment, and looked towards the outside of the city in the distance.

“As the saying goes, people may be afraid of ghosts, but ghosts are significantly more afraid of people. There is another saying: ‘if you don’t do anything wrong, you won’t fear ghosts knocking at your door.’ There is truth in both of these sayings, and both mostly refer to these helpless wandering ghosts. Unless they hold a deep grudge, they would dissipate in a single moment of carelessness. People have nothing to fear, unless one is extremely weak.”

As he spoke, Ji Yuan gazed in the direction of the city. He had some understanding of the composition of the population of ghosts in this city of the dead. On the southern border of the country of Zuyue, at the very least, there was no shortage of wandering ghosts.

‘But I cannot be certain if this is good or bad!’

Aside from this group of wandering ghosts who were sent into the city, there were no other disturbances until just before dawn. By the time Yan Fei was dozing off, the ghost city gradually faded until it vanished from sight.

Yan Fei was so startled that he lost his previous sleepiness.

“Mr. Ji, Elder Brother Niu, is the city gone?”

“It’s still there. You can see that the dark clouds have not dissipated. It’s just that with the sun is shining in the daytime, and the Yang energy is mixed in. The city has used a ghostly technique to create a place between Yin and Yang to conceal itself.”

Ji Yuan explained, standing up. He strode down the hill first, and Niu Batian and Yan Fei hurriedly followed.

It didn’t take long for the three of them to arrive at the location where the city had been. The surrounding area seemed more desolate than other places, and there were no tall trees. However, from Yan Fei’s point of view, there seemed to be nothing special as he traveled through here.

“Mr. Ji, look over there!”

Niu Batian pointed into the distance, and then ran quickly ahead. Ji Yuan and Yan Fei followed closely behind. About a li away, behind several large pieces of rubble, the three of them found a carriage.

This carriage was not only exquisitely designed, but was also very spacious. It required at least two horses pull it. However, there was no one currently in the carriage, nor were there horses at the front.

Niu Batian reached out his hand to touch the carriage, then lifted the curtain to take a look. He turned back to Ji Yuan.

“Mr. Ji, it’s quite clean, and it doesn’t look like it was abandoned…”

At this moment, Niu Batian suddenly noticed that Yan Fei’s gaze was strange. He frowned and asked:

“Brother Yan, what’s wrong?”

Yan Fei looked at Niu Batian and then at Ji Yuan, then carefully observed the carriage again, and replied with some uncertainty.

“This carriage… looks like the one I saw last night!”

“Are you sure?”

Niu Batian was taken aback for a moment, then suddenly thought of something.

“No wonder you could see the carriage headed towards the city: this carriage was not for ghosts, but for mortals!”

“What about the people in the carriage?”

Yan Fei asked subconsciously, gaze shifting between Niu Batian and Ji Yuan.

Ji Yuan also stared at the carriage with a frown. He inspected the empty interior of the carriage, then looked at the empty harness at the front, devoid of horses.

“Then I’m afraid they stayed in that ghost city…”

Niu Batian’s eyes lit up, and he immediately spoke in a righteous manner.

“Mr. Ji, these people have entered the ghost city without realizing. I’m afraid they might not be able to get out, and there is an very high likelihood that they will be discovered as mortals. It is extremely dangerous to be surrounded by ghosts. We should bring them out of the city of the dead. If we encounter any evil ghosts, we can just get rid of them on the way!”

Ji Yuan had indeed been thinking about the feasibility of this approach. When he heard Niu Batian’s words, he looked at him and smiled.

“You’re quite kindhearted, aren’t you?”3

“This is the chivalrous spirit I learned from Brother Yan. Although I may be a demon, I still have some humanity! Mr. Ji, what do you think?”

“Hmm… what about Warrior Yan?”

Ji Yuan looked at Yan Fei. The latter had his sword in his hand, his gaze shifting from the carriage to Ji Yuan. He nodded and said:

“If I happened to encounter a carriage here, I might not necessarily have paid it any attention. However, last night I saw it heading towards the city. Now that I have learned the truth, I feel a sense of responsibility. If Mr. Ji and Elder Brother Niu don’t mind, please take me with you!”

“Hahaha, no problem, no problem. Brother Yan’s martial arts are unparalleled. How can ordinary ghosts be your match? And if you encounter a powerful one, this Old Bull and Mr. Ji will be there!”

Niu Batian was secretly delighted. Given the current situation, Mr. Ji would most likely agree to enter the city of the dead.

Sure enough, Ji Yuan immediately expressed his opinion.

“In that case, let’s head into the city to look for them. I have dealt with the ghosts and gods of the underworld several times. Under normal circumstances, the ghosts in the city of the dead would be indistinguishable to ordinary people. Around a living person, the vitality of living Yang would be suppressed by the Yin energy. If they don’t get too close to any ghosts, they may not be noticed. They may not even realize that they are in a city of the dead at this moment.”4

“Then… do we need to wait until dark for the city to appear?”

Niu Batian deliberately asked. Although he did not find a suitable way to enter the city at present, he believed that Mr. Ji would definitely have a way. Of course, he could also try to attack and destroy it with demonic methods regardless of the consequences, but this would definitely not be appropriate.

“Delaying for that long would be dangerous for them. We must try to enter now.”

Ji Yuan currently had two options. The first was to directly use his immortal sword to cleave through the ghost realm’s Yin-Yang barrier. This wasn’t truly the underworld, after all; with the Green Vine Sword, this would be easy, but would likely alert some powerful ghosts.

The second method was to attempt to mimic the nighttime environment and integrate themselves into the ghost city. This was contingent upon the fact that this city of the dead’s barriers were far less refined than those of an immortal sect or other locations, and more susceptible to the influence of the outside world. In other words, he might be able to “trick” the ghost town itself.

With this thought, Ji Yuan no longer hesitated. With a single thought, his inner world materialized. Under his control, an infinite realm of mountains and rivers unfolded.

To Niu Batian and Yan Fei, it seemed as though the world around them had suddenly shifted. They felt the illusion of their own figures being pulled infinitely away, as if they were in another world, glimpsing the mountains and rivers from an endless distance.

This feeling was so intense and captivating that, in midst of their trance, Ji Yuan’s mind shifted, and he turned and pointed to the sky of his mental world.

In an instant, numerous chess pieces, both solid and intangible, lit up, resembling brilliantly shining stars in the sky. The color of the world changed, from white to black…

At this moment, scenery began to materialize around them: streets, houses, pedestrians… It gradually shifted from illusion to reality.

Yan Fei was simply amazed, while Niu Batian was dumbfounded and breathless.

‘As the stars shift, the world changes…’

T/n: JY be like, see, I could just cut through the barriers. It would be super easy, and like 99% of other xianxia protagonists would do the same. Or… Or… hear me out… I could literally bend reality to my will

This isn’t an exact translation: 改天 (gǎitiān): another day, 换 (huàn): to exchange/to switch, 地 (dì): ground/place/land. Literal TL is “change the land to another day,” which isn’t exactly correct in context (as we see by the end of the chapter), so I tweaked it

The Lantern Festival is celebrated on the 15th day of the first lunar month (February or early March) as the last day of traditional Chinese New Year celebrations.

T/n: Lol JY with the sarcasm

To clarify, JY is saying that the Yang energy given off by normal living people would be tamped down by the Yin of the ghost city, so no one would be able to sense their Yang unless they were very close by.

Chapter 305: Actually a Living Person!

The instant the surrounding city changed from illusory to reality, Ji Yuan immediately withdrew his Transformation of Heaven and Earth technique, and his inner world dissipated in that same moment, a process faster than the blink of an eye.

To Yan Fei, the scene he had just witnessed was merely miraculous, but to Niu Batian, it was beyond shocking.

Even if it was only for a moment, the impression of changing the world from day to night, of a world transformed, was simply too powerful.

The Old Bull suddenly recalled a memory of that night outside Nandao County: the moment the stinky woman recognized Mr. Ji, her demonic aura was in disarray, and she didn’t even dare to move.

Now, Niu Batian could somewhat empathize and understand her reaction. That stinky woman undoubtedly understood Mr. Ji’s abilities far better than he himself did. After realizing who he was, she was practically paralyzed with fear.

The Old Bull’s thoughts were fleeting. Despite the intensity, he also knew that it was not appropriate to question Mr. Ji directly.

The trio’s attention then turned to the surrounding city sights.

It was a gloomy daytime light. They currently stood in a place similar to a backyard stable. From the architecture to the surrounding sights, it was identical to an ordinary city, aside from the fact that the carriage had not come with them when they entered the city.

“It’s incredible! Aside from the strong Yin Qi, everything else is indistinguishable from a real city!”

Niu Batian praised. Yan Fei was naturally speechless, but Ji Yuan thought for a moment, then said:

“To a certain extent, this is indeed a real city. Let’s go find our people first. It shouldn’t be too hard to find living people in a city of the dead, if nothing happened to them.”

As Ji Yuan spoke, he opened his supernatural eyes wide, and channeled his spiritual energy to assist his vision, observing the ebb and flow of the vast Yin energy in the city.

As they exited the backyard and walked out into the street, they could already hear the sounds of a bustling city. Before them lay a sparsely populated street. In Ji Yuan’s vision, he could make out individuals’ real appearances and causes of death, while Yan Fei saw them as ordinary people.

Even without Niu Batian’s help, Yan Fei could clearly see the people and objects in this city. This was because the Yang fire of his body was suppressed by the yin energy in the city, making him temporarily partly Yin.

“Mr. Ji…they won’t notice our differences, will they?”

Yan Fei asked cautiously. Ji Yuan answered calmly.

“Warrior Yan, please don’t worry. I have applied a technique on you. Ordinary ghosts won’t be able to see through it.”

Yan Fei nodded, but still tightened his grip on his sword. After all, no matter how brave he was, knowing that all the people he saw in front of him were ghosts, he couldn’t help but feel a little nervous.

Ji Yuan glanced at Yan Fei’s sword, and spoke again.

“The sword you wield has taken countless lives. It holds within it a hidden ruthlessness, a sharp, life-reaping blade. Even a ghost would be injured if struck by it. Warrior Yan, you should be familiar with the story of the swordsman Du Yutian slaying ghosts while drunk, no?”1

Yan Fei’s eyes immediately lit up at Ji Yuan’s words. His greatest fear was for his swordsmanship to be completely ineffective against ghosts, tantamount to fighting an unbeatable foe. Since Mr. Ji claimed that he could harm ghosts, his spirits naturally lifted.

Niu Batian glanced at the passersby, and then into the distance. Even during the day, the place was still festooned with lanterns. There were vibrant lights and decorations everywhere.

“It looks like it should be very lively here…”

“It’s daytime at the moment!”

With Ji Yuan’s words, Niu Batian suddenly understood.

. . .

In an inn in the city, four people were struggling to get out of bed. They were two men and two women, but since the inn was short of space, they had only booked two separate rooms.

One of the men struggled out of bed, rubbing his forehead as he glanced as his friend sleeping on the other side, who also appeared to be listless.

They had slept so badly this past night that their back ached. They woke up feeling groggy and dizzy, and it took them a while to recover.

“Phew… What a lousy inn! I slept so poorly, we might as well have slept in the carriage in the wilderness.”

“I thought I was the only one who didn’t sleep well, but you feel the same?”

“Let’s go, let’s get up and find a good restaurant to eat at. We’ve been on the road in the wilderness for a long time, and I’ve been craving a good meal.”

After getting dressed, the two men walked to the door of their female traveling companions’ room, and knocked on the door.

“Knock, knock, knock…”

“Third Sister, Fourth Sister, are you up yet? It’s time to eat!”

“Knock, knock, knock…”

“We’re going to eat, let’s go get a good meal. We need to find some good horses later.”

After they had suddenly discovered a city last night, this group, which had been traveling through the wilderness for quite some time, were overjoyed, and hurriedly steered the carriage over to see if they could enter. To their surprise, this city’s gates were actually open at night.

Moreover, after entering the city, they found that it was very lively and bustling with activity, with a dense crowd, making it difficult for the carriage and horses to maneuver. The horses, however, seemed to be frightened upon entering the city, lurching and jerking, making it impossible for them to navigate the streets.

Thus, the group left their carriage and horses at a stable near the city gate. Unexpectedly, as soon as they arrived and untied the reins, the normally docile horses suddenly bolted like mad, bursting out of the stables, unable to be stopped.

This incident could be considered nothing less than an unforeseen disaster. The startled horses disappeared without a trace, costing them their purchase, but they fortunately didn’t hit anyone. The group could only conclude that they were unlucky. Exhausted, they found an inn in the city to rest.

Naturally, they would have to buy new horses today, as their chances of finding the frightened horses were slim.

The two men waited outside for a long time before the women finally emerged and greeted them.

“Good morning, Second Brother. Good morning, Young Master Zhou.”

“Good morning, ladies!”

Zhou Xing immediately returned the greeting.

“Oh, you’re dragging on! Stop the formalities, let’s go find a nice restaurant.”

Ke Yudong, the man outside, urged repeatedly. He led the three of them out of the inn. As they went downstairs, the innkeeper was still flipping through the account book. Seeing them come down, he smiled and asked:

“Gentlemen, did you sleep well last night?”

“What shitty nonsense, it was horrible!”

The innkeeper didn’t take it seriously, but instead offered a kind reminder.

“At first, I was surprised when you said that you would sleep overnight. What kind of sleep could you even get? No wonder you didn’t sleep well!”

His words left the four people currently walking down the stairs somewhat baffled.

“You don’t sleep at night, but sleep during the day? That’s very strange, innkeeper!”

“Of course I do, but there are plenty who don’t. Guests, what are you doing…”

The innkeeper looked the four of them over, but they didn’t bother to pay him any more attention, and simply walked out of the inn together.

Outside, the overcast weather made the scenery gloomy and unpleasant. Glancing around outside, they saw that the streets were practically deserted.

“How strange! There were so many people last night, how come there aren’t that many during the day? Could it be that people here really sleep during the day?”

Zhou Xing couldn’t help but wonder.

“Maybe it was some kind of folk festival last night, and everyone stayed out late, so they all slept in today?”

One of the women suggested.

“Perhaps. Let’s go, let’s find a restaurant to eat at!”

The four of them didn’t bother much with anything more. Without further ado, they set out to search the city. Before long, they found a place called Tengyuan Restaurant.2

It looked decent, but there weren’t many diners inside, and not even a handful of waiters. However, this meant that the kitchen could prioritize cooking for them, so the four of them didn’t mind.

However, after the food was served and they tried a bite, Ke Yundong couldn’t help but grow angry.

The six dishes came out quickly, as if they had been brought directly from the kitchen. They tasted the dishes immediately after their names were announced, but they found every single dish hard to swallow.

With a bang, Ke Yundong slammed his chopsticks on the table.

“Shopkeeper, what are you doing? Are these dishes even edible? This wine is bland as water, these dishes are full of strange smells, and it is almost like this food has gone bad. Do you lack money, or ingredients? You have the nerve to keep your shop open and do business after serving these kinds of things? No wonder no one comes to your door!”

Seeing the guests growing angry, the diners at a few of the other tables glanced over to this side. The shopkeeper and the waiter hurried over, but merely frowned at some of the language the guests used, without thinking too deeply about it.

“Hey, sir, calm down, calm down. This dish is indeed quite different from what ordinary people would cook, but we don’t have anyone here to pay us tribute. The really good chefs would all be with the nobles in the city. You will just have to make do with this. In fact, the taste is not bad!”

The shopkeeper smiled widely, bowed, and apologized.

Ke Yundong was irritated. What was this nonsense the shopkeeper was saying?

“Pay tribute? Do you think your small restaurant is an imperial city, or the grave of some newly deceased? Someone would pay you tribute? Are you kidding me?”

As soon as these words were spoken, the shopkeeper’s smile slowly disappeared. The diners around them who were originally ignoring this table all turned their heads in unison. All the “people” in the building, including the waiter, stared at the four people at the table. The look in their eyes gave Ke Yundong and the others chills.

“What is this, what? Are you some kind of shady shop? In broad daylight, you’re still trying to bully customers? Be careful, I will report you to the police! Brother Zhou, Fourth Sister!”

“Don’t worry, I’m here!”

Zhou Xing clenched his fists, frowning as he stared around. Ke Yunqin, the fourth daughter of the Ke family, also tensed. The two of them were both martial artists, and in situations like this, everyone relied on them.

The shopkeeper said nothing, but instead walked over to the table, picked up Ke Yundong’s chopsticks, and brought them to his nose, sniffing them. A half-smiling, almost sinister expression appeared on his face.

“Hehehehe… Hey, hey… they are living people!”

“Living people!?”

“They’re alive?”

“How did the living get in?”

“From their words just now, that does seem to be the case!”

“Hehehehe, living people…”

“It’s been a while…”

The comments from the surrounding diners were chilling. Many of the diners had stood up.

“Really, how funny… Are you really going to joke about you all being dead?”

Ke Yundong stammered, but as soon as he finished speaking, the four of them paled.

They watched the shopkeeper lick his lips with a foot-long tongue and laugh.

“Sir, it is you who must be joking. We truly are all dead…”

“Hehehehe…”

“Hahahahaha…”

“The yang energy of a living person!”

“The blood of the living is wonderful!”

“And the heart and liver!”

“I want a taste too!”

These diners, who would come to this restaurant to eat during the day, could be considered “foodies.” Now, their faces had turned horrifying. Some had cracked mouths, others had unnaturally long tongues, some had withered faces, and others had long, black nails.

“What is all this killing intent over here? Do you dare to disobey the law of this city?”

A man in black robe had just walked into the restaurant when he saw this scene, and immediately gave an angry roar.

The shopkeeper hurriedly approached and whispered an explanation, pointing to the four pale-faced people at the table. The more the black-robed man listened, the more his eyes gleamed.

“They are actually alive? Perfect! Since they have stumbled into the city, they cannot blame us. I will take these four people away. They will be perfect as the main course for the City Lord at tonight’s banquet!”

“Run!”

Bang!

Zhou Xing roared and immediately overturned the table.

But in the very next moment, the black-robed man transformed into a dark shadow and wrapped around Zhou Xing, instantly making him feel as though he had fallen into an icy cave. One of the long-nailed hands had already pinched his neck, rendering him unable to move, while the other hand stretched out several feet and also pinched Ke Yunqin’s neck.

“Hahahahahahaha… Wonderful, wonderful! This body temperature, this breath, they are indeed alive, and two are martial artists at that! Hahahahahaha…”

The black-robed man opened his mouth wide, revealing his teeth and pale cheekbones.

Ke Yundong and Ke Yunxin were so frightened that they collapsed to the ground. They couldn’t even walk, let alone try to resist.

This does not appear to be a folktale or story from our world, I couldn’t find any references. THANK YOU Aersene for pointing out that this story was mentioned in chapter 49

腾 (téng): to soar/to hover/to vacate, 远 (yuǎn): far/distant/remote OR 远 (yuàn): to distance oneself from –> “Flying Far Away Restaurant,” suitable name for a city of the dead

Chapter 306: Who Is He, Truly?

The black-robed man laughed heartily. Stumbling across a few living people today really solved his urgent problem.

Zhou Xing and Ke Yunqin were pinched by the ghost’s claws, and immediately passed out. The black-robed man looked at the two people who had collapsed on the ground. Both of them had been frightened to the point of incontinence, both by him and by the ghosts around them. However, he did not feel disgusted.

“Haha, at least their flesh is tender and delicate!”

The black-robed man was very satisfied with the four people, and was about to take them away.

“Sir, sir, these people came to eat in my restaurant. We found them first. Can’t you leave those two on the ground to us? You can’t just take them all…”

The shopkeeper of the restaurant couldn’t but want to fight for them, but before he could say anything more, the black-robed man glared at him with dark eyes.

“You dare to argue with me? Beware, lest your soul be rent asunder!”

With these words, the black-robed figure abandoned the argument, not even attempting to teach the surrounding ghosts a lesson. Instead, his arms stretched out like serpents, wrapping around the group of four, binding them and directly dragging them out of the restaurant before walking away.

Inside the restaurant, everyone, from the shopkeeper to the other diners, could only watch helplessly, the expressions on their face contorted into hideous grimaces.

Seeing the black-robed figure lead his mortal bodies away without any scruples, the shopkeeper’s rage erupted, and he instantly transformed into a thin, green-tinged, fanged demon.

“Even if you are a Dark Minister,1 that doesn’t mean you can be so arrogant, right?”

The echo of the ghost’s roar rattled the inn’s cups. The black-robed figure on the street outside sneered back at the group of ghosts in the building.

“Okay, I’ll give you a chance. Come out and rob me.”

Many of the ghosts in the inn revealed murderous expressions, their eyes fixed grimly on the black-robed figure in the street. In this city of the dead, where they could only enter but never leave, the allure of four living people was too great.

The black-robed figure waited with a cold gaze. He flung the four unconscious figures into the street. A few breaths passed, but the ghosts within the restaurant still hadn’t burst out. The black-robed figure lifted his robe, revealing a special, jet-black soul-binding rope wrapped around his waist.

“Hmph, a bunch of trash dare to make such a fuss!”

With a cold snort, the black-robed man grabbed two people in each hand, lifted them up, and transformed into a ghostly shadow, disappearing down the street.

The group of ghosts within the restaurant were enraged. The shopkeeper, furious, roared, his hair and beard flying in all directions.

“Aaargh— Abusing the position of Dark Minister to bully us ghosts far too much!”

. . .

Elsewhere in the ghost city, Ji Yuan, Niu Batian, and Yan Fei had just finished their visit to one inn, and were preparing to move on to the next.

The two inns closest to the stables from which they had emerged were the most likely candidates to find the carriage’s owners.

But at this moment, Ji Yuan suddenly heard an echoing roar.

“Aaargh— Abusing the position of Dark Minister to bully us ghosts far too much… bully us ghosts far too much…”

The moment he heard these words, it was like a ripple passed through his mind, and he froze in his tracks. Following this sudden thought, he stretched out his hand and briefly pinched his fingers together. His brow furrowed.

“Mr. Ji, what’s the matter?”

“This way, they’re in trouble!”

At that, Ji Yuan’s feet moved as fast as lightning. He turned and swiftly headed elsewhere, and Niu Batian and Yan Fei quickly followed.

This city of the dead was much larger than expected, far larger than cities like those in Nandao County. Countless old ghosts lingered here, and there were likely a number of ghost cultivators lurking about. Ji Yuan even felt the sense that it was far more powerful than any underworld he had visited before, akin to a sacred ground for ghost cultivation.

Therefore, even with a bull demon with a high level of cultivation, Ji Yuan didn’t dare to stand out too much, let alone rashly perform his flight technique.

In a one-on-one fight, Ji Yuan could handle all kinds of immortals, demons, and gods without fear. However, if the number of opponents exceeded a certain limit, it would become very dangerous for him.

When Ji Yuan and the other two arrived outside of Tengyuan Restaurant, they could see a fierce and resentful aura in the building, even though they were still out on the street. The waiter in the inn was propping up an overturned table. There were some dishes and scraps of vegetables on the ground, as well as two small puddles of urine that exuded a faint stink.

“Mr. Ji, it seems that we are a little late?”

Niu Batian leaned closer to Ji Yuan and whispered. The latter nodded, but continued to walk towards the restaurant. His voice rang out before he even arrived.

“Ooh… what happened here? Was there a fight?”

The shopkeeper’s appearance had returned to normal, but his resentment still lingered. When he heard the voice outside the door, he replied coldly.

“The Lord Dark Minister has such an inflated arrogance! Four living idiots came to the restaurant today. We discovered them first, but he snatched them away by virtue of his power. He didn’t even let them settle their bill!”

The shopkeeper sighed as he spoke, gathering his composure and trying to put on a smile.

“However, if the guests wish to eat, please come in.”

“Oh? How could that be? How can living people come here? That’s quite unusual. Did the Dark Minister kidnap them to absorb their vital Yang energy himself?”

Ji Yuan spoke with a surprised tone, and entered the room to chat with the shopkeeper.

“Ah, it’s indeed unusual, but this time he didn’t snatch it away to eat it himself. Tonight is a grand celebratory banquet held by the City Lord in honor of his successful milestone in cultivation. The Dark Minister planned to offer these living people to the City Lord first. Now I don’t even know where he hid those living bodies. Hmph, it is precisely out of respect for the City Lord that we stay our hand, otherwise, we would have fought with this Dark Minister without fear!”

The shopkeeper narrowed his eyes to reveal a faint light. After uttering some harsh words, he thought of something.

“Oh, by the way, the guests, you wish to eat, we… uh…”

The guests he had been talking to had vanished.

“Where are they? When did you see them leave?”

Several waiters around shook their heads.

“I didn’t notice. They disappeared in the blink of an eye.”

On the street, Ji Yuan and his companions naturally went to look for the so-called Dark Minister. However, the Dark Minister, apparently fearing that someone else might be targeting him, had done an excellent job of concealing himself.

At least, Ji Yuan’s divinations were unable to pinpoint his exact location. It was even more difficult to pinpoint any possible sources of Yang energy within the city.

‘This is getting a little tricky! ‘

In the current situation, there were only two options: the first was to turn the City of the Dead upside down and forcibly find the Dark Minister and his unfortunate captives; the other was to wait until the Ghost City Lord’s banquet and demand the release of those individuals.

The former was like giving a slap in the face: one misstep, and the entire city of the dead would turn hostile. The latter was equally humiliating: one misstep could still make the entire city of the dead turn hostile.

Yan Fei was a mortal: although he realized that things are going badly, he still couldn’t fully grasp the situation. Niu Batian, on the other hand, had already grasped the complexity of the current situation.

“Mr. Ji, what should we do? How about we just go wild!”

The Old Bull now had a kind of blind faith in Ji Yuan. He even felt that if Mr. Ji wished to, he could use his immense supernatural power to directly alter the world, allowing the heavenly celestial righteous Yang2 to directly flood the ghost city, or even directly sever the ghost city’s foundation. Who would dare to provoke such a dangerous person?

“Go wild? Who would we go wild at? The whole City of the Dead? Wouldn’t it be difficult for you, a barbaric bull, to take them all on?”

Ji Yuan asked back. Niu Batian scratched his head and said.

“Although this Old Bull might be a bit conceited, I am aware that I do not know the depth of power of the ghosts in this city, and likely would not be able to handle it. But with your abilities, sir, I don’t believe that anyone in this ghost city could stop us!”

“You…”

Before Ji Yuan could say “have such a glib tongue,” he suddenly saw a monstrous aura rising from the direction of the city gate.

“Someone has come knocking at the gate?”3

Niu Batian asked excitedly, but Ji Yuan shook his head to refute.

“No. This monstrous aura, though strong, is not aggressive. It seems to be just a means of showing status. I remember that the shopkeeper mentioned that tonight was some kind of celebration. They may be guests attending the banquet!”

Ji Yuan explained to his two companions as the three of them headed over, intent on seeing what kind of monster had arrived.

Judging from the current situation, if the guest’s monstrous aura was so strong, then this City Lord was definitely no simple individual, which made Ji Yuan even more cautious.

A special carriage shrouded in mist and covered in fish scales slowly drove along the city’s main road. The vehicle was not pulled by horses, but rather by a four-legged aquatic demon beast. The vapor from its breath drifted to the rear, making the entire procession appear as if shrouded in a haze.

In addition to this large carriage, the procession was accompanied by several attendants to the front and back, some carrying weapons, and others carrying boxes. Each exuded a demonic or monstrous aura, making the scene quite ostentatious.

It was currently daytime, so there weren’t that many ghosts wandering the city. Many ghosts had originally wanted to approach, but the strong monstrous aura kept them at bay.

Inside the carriage sat a handsome man and woman. After entering the city, each of them peered through the beaded curtains to either side to observe the passing sights.

“I must say, this Endless Ghost City does have some secrets. I wonder how much Old Ghost Wuya has improved after this breakthrough?”

“He is still a ghost, after all!”

“You can’t say that, it’s true that a ghost body has flaws, but even then…”

The man’s paused at this point.

“Even then what?”

The woman on one side turned to look at him curiously, only to find his eyes wide open, staring blackly ahead, gaze shifting with the carriage’s progress.

“Stop!”

The man suddenly shouted, startling the woman next to him.

“Get out of the carriage with me, get out of the carriage with me! I didn’t expect Old Ghost Wuya to invite even him!”

The man grabbed the woman’s hand with an impatient voice and dragged her out of the carriage with him.

To the side of the procession, Niu Batian frowned and looked at the nearby carriage, tilting his head to ask Ji Yuan.

“Mr. Ji, why did this carriage stop so abruptly?”

The Old Bull had barely finished speaking when a man and a woman in brocade robes disembarked the carriage and hurriedly strode over towars him. When they were about ten zhang away from him, they bowed respectfully at a ninety-degree angle4 in his direction.

“Honored Sir, I am Gao Tianming of Tianshui Lake, accompanied by my wife Xia Qiu. I pay my respects to Mr. Ji!”

Ji Yuan frowned, pondering for a moment, before a look of realization dawned on his face. He simply bowed with cupped hands in return.

“So it was Mr. Gao of Tianshui Lake. I remember you attended the Old Dragon’s birthday celebration at the Tongtian River in Dazhen?”

“Indeed! Mr. Ji, you actually still remember me, I am truly honored! I wouldn’t dare be presumptuous to you, sir. Please, just call me by my name. This is my humble wife, also an aquatic creature, not yet transformed into a flood dragon!”

Niu Batian suddenly felt his scalp tingling, and Yan Fei to the side felt similarly.

‘Hey… just who exactly is Mr. Ji?’

黑 (hēi): black/dark/sinister/secret/concealed, 令 (lìng): to order/to command/government position. Literally like the Men in Black!!! 😀

T/n: One heck of a mouthful

i.e. come looking for trouble, like asking “is there going to be a fight?”

Deep, very respectful bow

Chapter 307: The Unfathomable Mr. Ji

Having met Gao Tianming and his wife, Ji Yuan now had some new ideas in mind. After accepting the greetings, he smiled and spoke to them.

“Alright, there’s no need to be so polite. It is a blessing to meet you here.”

Ji Yuan extended his hand to the side and introduced his companions.

“This is the great Hero1 Yan Fei, and this is the demon Niu Batian.”

Gao Tianming and Lady Gao bowed to the two of them.

“Good to meet the both of you!”

Niu Batian and Yan Fei hurriedly returned the greetings, and they all briefly acquainted themselves.

Gao Tianming was extremely surprised to see Ji Yuan here. He hadn’t had the chance to speak with Ji Yuan at the Dragon Lord’s birthday banquet, so being able to chat with him now on the streets of the city of the dead was naturally a welcome development.

“Mr. Ji, you also know Xin Wuya? Did he invite you to attend his celebration?”

“Xin Wuya? Is that the name of the Lord of this ghost city?”

Ji Yuan replied after a moment’s thought.

Gao Tianming could infer from this statement that Mr. Ji and the Lord of the ghost city did not in fact know each other.

“Did City Lord Xin not invite you? Did you come to congratulate him on your own initiative, sir?”

Gao Tianming’s expression was visibly surprised, while at the same time internally complaining to himself.

‘This Xin Wuya can garner such great respect? Could he truly make Mr. Ji come over to congratulate him in person?’

“Hmph, what congratulations? Mr. Ji and the two of us found a carriage-load of idiots had strayed into the city of the dead, so we wanted to come in to find and rescue them. Otherwise, this place full of old ghosts would be very agitated at the sight of a couple of living people.”

Niu Batian sneered coldly, explaining Ji Yuan’s reasoning while at the same time elevating his status in their eyes by showing off how well he and Mr. Ji knew each other.

“Oh? Is that so? So Mr. Ji caught that old ghost Wuya doing such a thing. If he had any sense, he would have handed them over. I don’t believe that he would dare do such evil acts in front of Mr. Ji!”

Indeed, they heard right: he used “City Lord Xin Wuya” previously, but now, hearing that the other party might be at odds with Mr. Ji, he had directly switched to “old ghost Wuya.”

Gao Tianming and his wife, Xia Qiu, possessed considerable monstrous spiritual energy, and had been specially invited to the celebration. They must have had connections with the City Lord of the ghost city, and their presence together likely signaled their recognition of the city of the dead’s status.

Yet, even under such circumstances, upon hearing Mr. Ji’s intentions, they immediately and unhesitatingly pledged their support.

Their actions further reinforced Mr. Ji’s profound strength in Niu Batian and Yan Fei’s eyes.

Ji Yuan nodded.

“The City Lord of the ghost city likely doesn’t know about this matter yet. As far as I know, those people were captured by a ghost called the Dark Minister, to be handed over to the Lord during the banquet. I was originally considering ways to properly rescue them, but since you and your wife are here, there is some room to maneuver. We can try to petition for their release.”

Gao Tianming narrowed his eyes, gazing into the depths of the city.

“Mr. Ji, don’t worry, I will take care of this matter. I know this old ghost Wuya well, I doubt he will refuse to hand over the prisoners!”

At this, Gao Tianming smiled again, and bowed slightly before ushering them towards his carriage.

“Mr. Ji, please take a seat, let’s go find that old ghost Wuya together!”

It seemed that Gao Tianming was well-acquainted with the City Lord of the ghost city, so Ji Yuan could rest assured. It would be best to avoid taking any action, of course. However, he also didn’t want to ride in that damp-looking carriage, which would have been unpalatable to any non-aquatic creatures.

“No need, I can simply walk. You two can take a seat.”

“In that case, I will walk with you, Mr. Ji!”

Gao Tianming declared, waving a hand towards the carriage. The procession began moving again, but this time the carriage was empty. The very man the procession was serving was walking beside it, chatting and laughing with the man in white.

Ji Yuan politely chatted with Gao Tianming and his wife for a little bit, focusing mainly on the Old Dragon’s affairs. After that, he simply listened, and didn’t say anything more. It wasn’t that he was acting aloof, but rather that he simply lacked common ground to chat about.

Although Gao Tianming desperately wanted to establish a good relationship with Ji Yuan, he knew that in front of such a distinguished figure, he shouldn’t approach too aggressively. Thus, seeing that Mr. Ji was merely listening calmly, occasionally offering a polite smile, he quickly withdrew and had a maid — a water monster in disguise — bring a tray of preserved fruits and pastries to Ji Yuan’s side.

“Mr. Ji, please enjoy. I will go and greet the two friends behind us.”

“Alright.”

Ji Yuan responded lightly, and picked up a slice of dried apricots as a gesture of appreciation for his enthusiasm.

Gao Tianming winked at his wife, Xia Qiu, then walked back a few steps with a smile, naturally approaching Niu Batian and Yan Fei. He bowed slightly and whispered:

“Excuse me, gentlemen, I was a little engrossed in chatting with Mr. Ji just now.”

Yan Fei quickly returned the bow, followed by Niu Batian. Niu Batian2 swallowed the remaining red bean cake in his mouth and replied understandingly.

“It’s okay, it’s okay, this Old Bull understands how you feel, after all, it’s not easy to meet Mr. Ji!”

“Brother Niu is right! I wonder how Hero Yan and Brother Niu met Mr. Ji?”

Yan Fei smiled helplessly, gripping his sword.

“Master Gao, you praise me too much. I am just a mere mortal warrior, I don’t deserve the title of Hero. I was saved by Mr. Ji a little over ten years ago, and he saved my life again a few days ago. It’s not a matter of friendship, but rather of life-saving grace!”

Gao Tianming nodded after hearing this, but didn’t dare to neglect his manners.

“So that’s how it is. Hero Yan and Mr. Ji have known each other for more than ten years. For a mortal, this is quite a long span of time! What about you, Brother Niu?”

As for Yan Fei’s refusal to be called a Hero, Gao Tianming pretended he hadn’t heard it. Mr. Ji called him by that title, so how could he call him Xiao Yan or Xiao Fei?3

“Oh, just call me Old Bull. Haha, actually it’s similar to Brother Yan. I was previously chased by a powerful enemy, but I didn’t expect Brother Yan to be caught in the middle, and involve Mr. Ji along the way. As a result, that stinky bitch recognized Mr. Ji and was terrified…”

The Old Bull gave a brief account of what happened that day, making Gao Tianming once again exclaim about his good fortune.

Having finished his story, Niu Batian looked at Ji Yuan in front of them, took a step closer to Gao Tianming, and said in a low voice.

“Brother Gao, what is Mr. Ji’s background, and how powerful is he?”

Gao Tianming also subconsciously glanced at Ji Yuan’s back. Even in this city of the dead, he felt a special sense of calm when looking at him.

“Mr. Ji’s identity is extremely mysterious, and I don’t know too much. If you really want to know, you can probably ask the Dragon Lord. I first met him at the Dragon Lord’s thousandth birthday banquet. Numerous aquatic creatures traveled thousands of li to congratulate him, and there’s a rumor…”

Gao Tianming’s voice trailed off again, then became even quieter.

“I heard His Royal Highness the Dragon Son mention it casually. It is said that the Dragon Lord almost missed his own birthday banquet back then because he spent years searching for his friend, Mr. Ji, to invite him to the feast. The countless aquatic creatures and realms that came to congratulate him were less important in his heart than Mr. Ji alone…”

“Hiss…”

Niu Batian’s head was numb, and Yan Fei had goosebumps all over his body at his words. It wasn’t fear, but rather an inexplicable excitement.

A Dragon Lord’s thousand-year birthday? Thousands of aquatic creatures from all over the world coming to celebrate? Just by hearing these words, he could already imagine the scene. In comparison, this ghost city’s so-called celebration was nothing.4

The more Gao Tianming observed Niu Batian and Yan Fei’s reactions, the more he understood that these two people were not that familiar with Mr. Ji. Yan Fei was a mortal, so it was natural that Mr. Ji would not discuss such matters with him. And the Old Bull seemed to only have met Mr. Ji very recently.

“As for Mr. Ji’s strength, I estimate that he is at least a True Immortal!”

“True Immortal!”

Niu Batian whispered subconsciously. This was truly a legendary figure.

“That’s right! He is least True Immortal! I am familiar with the Dragon Lord’s son, His Highness Ying. Although not many know of Mr. Ji outside of Dazhen, I have come to understand that Mr. Ji’s greatest skill is probably his swordsmanship, followed by his edicts. He also possessed the Spirit-Binding technique, and his words carry the sound of the Tao.”

Having finished this sentence, Gao Tianming grew even more engaged, and quickly added more.

“It’s hard to explain the rest, but Mr. Ji’s swordsmanship is incredible, even terrifying. I heard that Mr. Ji has a sword technique called the ‘Heaven Rending Sword.’ As the name suggests, when the sword is drawn, it carries enough might to rend the heavens. Without the ability to withstand the heavens, one cannot withstand the force of the Heaven-Rending Sword. When I first heard about it, I couldn’t even imagine it. His Highness Ying told me to imagine standing alone on the ground, the sky collapsing over my head…”

After hearing his words, even Yan Fei could imagine the power of this immortal technique. Both he and Niu Batian subconsciously glanced up at the sky, gulping involuntarily.

However, Niu Batian was quite surprised that he hadn’t heard anything about Mr. Ji’s fire control technique. Perhaps it couldn’t he compared to the might of the Heaven-Rending Sword, but it was not so insignificant to not even be mentioned. Last night, he had almost lost his life just by visualizing the flames.

“Uh, Brother Gao, what about Mr. Ji’s fire control technique? It’s so powerful, could you please tell us about it?”

Niu Batian originally wanted to know more, but he saw a look of confusion on Gao Tianming’s face.

“Well, I’ve never heard of Mr. Ji’s extraordinary fire control technique, nor have I ever seen him use it. Could it be that Brother Niu has seen it?”

“No way? You haven’t heard of it?”

“I’ve never heard of it. Brother Niu, do you know something?”

Niu Batian was stunned for a moment, and then felt inexplicably proud for some reason. However, his face remained serious as he spoke in a low voice.

“Actually, I didn’t see it in full, but it’s clear that Mr. Ji can control fire, and his abilities are definitely beyond Brother Gao’s imagination. Last night, Mr. Ji used a breath of fire to help this Old Bull eradicate that evil woman’s technique. It was just an exhale of fire…”

There was a hint of fear in the Old Bull’s words, setting the scene for what he was recounting.

“Although I didn’t see it, this Old Bull has keen spiritual senses. When that breath of fire entered my body, I could vaguely sense the boundless sea of flames behind it. That raging fire could surely consume anything…”

Niu Batian took a deep breath, half-deliberate, half-genuinely needed, creating a feeling of tension, before he continued.

“I once asked Mr. about the source of the fire. Mr. Ji said that it was the True Fire that he had cultivated, a skill no one else in the world possesses. It is a combination of the Divine Heart Fire that transforms into the True Fire of the Alchemy Furnace, the extreme Yang energy of the Celestial Fire of Samadhi, and the extreme Yin energy of the Cold Yin True Fire.3 These three are combined and refined into one, which is called… the True Fire of Samadhi!”5

Gao Tianming broke out in a cold sweat just by hearing those words. He was an aquatic creature, and water and fire were mutually restraining elements. Just hearing this explanation was extremely terrifying.

Yan Fei wasn’t as deeply impressed by the True Fire of Samadhi as he had been at the Heaven Rending Sword, but the Old Bull’s words reminded him of something, so he chimed in.

“Brother Niu, you forgot one more thing: Mr. Ji’s Golden-Armored Warrior!”

“That’s right! Brother Gao, you didn’t mention this method just now either. Mr. Ji uses a piece of yellow paper to summon a golden-armored guardian general, known as the Golden-Armored Warrior, or the Yellow-Robed Warrior. I asked Mr. Ji about it, and he said it could be considered a guardian technique. Although I haven’t seen this warrior in action, judging by its arrogant demeanor and its reverence of Mr. Ji, I imagine it’s certainly quite skilled!”

Gao Tianming absolutely agreed with this statement. How could one of Mr. Ji’s summons be weak?

But at the same time, Gao Tianming shook his head helplessly.

“I have indeed never heard of this technique…”

At this moment, when Niu Batian and Gao Tianming looked at Ji Yuan again, their sense of awe and unfathomable depths grew even more palpable. Gao Tianming felt that perhaps, next time he saw His Highness the Dragon Son, he would have something to talk about that was better than anything the other party had to share.

“By the way, Brother Gao, what would happen if the City Lord of the ghost city doesn’t hand over those people…”

The Old Bull spoke, with some malicious meaning hidden in his words. Gao Tianming also gave a sinister smile when he heard.

“Hmph, that would be courting death. As far as I knows, although Mr. Ji treats all sentient beings equally, he hates evil ones the most…!”
