Chapter 308: Turmoil

Niu Batian, Gao Tianming, and Yan Fei were all whispering to each other in the back of the procession. Yan Fei was calm, but the former two grew more and more excited as they chatted. However, they all know that Mr. Ji did not like to show off, so such talk was only suitable for their own private entertainment and speculation.

As the team progressed, they passed through half of the city before finally arriving at the city center. In front of them was the majestic City Lord’s Mansion.

From a distance, they could see a gatehouse only slightly smaller than that of the city gate outside. On it was an enormous plaque, with the name of the mansion written on it.

Ji Yuan murmured to himself:

“The Netherworld Ghost Palace?”1

Gao Tianming had now returned to standing beside Ji Yuan. He responded when he heard Ji Yuan’s words.

“Indeed. Old ghost Wuya is quite remarkable among ghost cultivators. Without any foundation in the divine path, he has grown even more powerful than many ghosts and gods. He is constantly taking in wandering spirits, and single-handedly built the Endless Ghost City.2 He taught some spirits ghost cultivation, with the ambition of creating a Sacred Land for ghosts.”

Ji Yuan nodded.

“He is quite ambitious.”

He could see some clues that supported this conclusion. This ghost city constantly took in wandering spirits, many of which had exhausted their Yin lifespan and would dissipate, enriching the city’s Yin Qi. This rich Yin Qi provided a favorable environment for some gifted ghosts, allowing them to break through their limits even without particularly outstanding ghost cultivation techniques.

This was actually a common phenomenon in some underworlds under the jurisdiction of the various city gods. Occasionally, there would be some ghosts in the underworld who transcended their Yin lifespans and survived, but these ghosts often harbored deep resentment and obsession. The underworld conducted an annual “household registration”3 review. If a ghost were to be found to have exceeded their Yin lifespan limit, and perceived as showing signs of trouble, the underworld officials would directly detain them and bring them before the heads of the relevant divisions, where they were often executed outright.

And in this Endless Ghost City, the City Lord was likely pleased to see ghosts rise to prominence. However, aside from those approved by the City Lord, no other ghosts were allowed to leave the city.

The front of the procession had already stopped, and one of the aquatic spirits shouted loudly.

“Master Gao of Tianshui Lake has arrived—!”

The ghost soldiers guarding the gate outside had already noticed the convoy. Many dignitaries who had come to congratulate the City Lord had also been arriving over the past couple days. Upon hearing he announced party’s name, they hurried forward.

“Master Gao, thank you for making the long journey. Please follow me to the other courtyard to rest. A servant will inform you when the banquet is ready for you to attend!”

With that, the ghost guards prepared to lead the way, clearly not intending to allow the procession to enter the palace directly. At this moment, Gao Tianming glanced at Ji Yuan and hurried forward.

“Wait!”

When he had been walking with Ji Yuan, Gao Tianming had deliberately suppressed his monstrous aura, but now it emanated again. As he approached the gatehouse, he brought an enormous feeling of oppression down upon the ghost guards.

“Master Gao, do you have any special requests? We will do our best to satisfy anything you ask!”

Gao Tianming smiled.

“Haha, it’s nothing special. Where is Xin Wuya now? I would like to meet with him and discuss something.”

The ghost soldier smiled apologetically.

“Please forgive me, Master Gao. The City Lord has important matters to attend to, so no one can see him at the moment. He will come out at the banquet tonight.”

“Oh? That old ghost Wuya is so busy? I can’t even meet him if I wants to?”

“That’s right, my husband and I have come all the way from Tianshui Lake. Upon arriving in the Endless Ghost City, we must first pay our respects to the City Lord. As the host, it is only natural to greet the guests first. This is how it should be!”

Xia Qiu also stepped forward, brimming with monstrous energy. Although she was not a dragon, she clearly possessed considerably strong cultivation. Meeting Mr. Ji today was their fate and fortune, and they were determined to leave a good impression. However, compared to Gao Tianming, Xia Qiu seemed much more subdued.

The ghost soldier hesitated and looked at Gao Tianming.

“Master Gao, can you please tell this humble one why you wish to see the City Lord? It would be best for me to have something to say when I go to report!”

This ghost guard appeared young, but he was actually an old ghost who had lived for a long time. He knew there must be a reason for Gao Tianming’s intent air.

Gao Tianming glanced back at Ji Yuan, saw the latter nod his head slightly, and then turned back to the ghost soldier.

“Several friends of mine accidentally entered the Ghost City last night. They are all living people. They were captured by a Dark Ministers from your city, and their whereabouts are unknown. It was rumored that they would be served to Xin Wuya as dishes at the banquet. I am here to claim them!”

Living people? Friends of the Flood Dragon of Tianshui Lake?

The surrounding ghost soldiers were slightly startled at these words.

“Then, please wait a moment, Master Gao, Lady Gao. I will report to the City Lord right away!”

The aura of the two monsters was so powerful that the ghost soldiers couldn’t bear the pressure and rushed inside to report.

The Netherworld Ghost Palace occupied a vast area. The ghost guard hurried inside to report, passing through door after door and corridor after corridor, before finally arriving outside a massive, pitch-black building.

The black door of this building was tightly locked, emanating bursts of materialized dark energy, and a chilling atmosphere that even ghosts found difficult to bear.

“My Lord, Master Gao of Tianshui Lake has arrived with Lady Gao, saying that they must pay you a visit.”

The ghost soldier waited for a long time, but there was no response from inside. He could only loudly report Gao Tianming’s matter from in front of the door.

“Master Gao of Tianshui Lake said that several of his mortal friends mistakenly strayed into the city last night and were captured by the Dark Minister. They are to be presented to the City Lord at the banquet. Master Gao hopes that the City Lord might release them immediately.”

Waves of eerie ghostly aura began to drift around, and phantom ghostly images emerged one after the other. Even the surrounding ghosts became nervous.

“Gao Tianming’s friends? Mortals?”

A voice rang out from the black building, making the ghost soldier breathe a sigh of relief.

“That is correct. Master Gao and Lady Gao seem to be quite angry about this.”

“Hmph, this is not Tianshui Lake. Go and tell him that if the Dark Minister truly brings a living person as tribute at the banquet, I will hand them over to him. I don’t have time to see him right now.”

“As the City Lord commands!”

The ghost soldier hurriedly saluted, and immediately took his leave.

Outside the mansion, Gao Tianming was surprised to receive this response after having waited for such for a long time, and his anger immediately flared.

“Old Ghost Wuya is so arrogant! Can’t he find that Dark Minister and demand the release of those men right now?”

“Master Gao, please do not begrudge us. The City Lord said that he was busy with important matters right now, so he cannot meet with you. He will release the mortals tonight, when the Dark Minister arrives!”

“Hmph!”

Gao Tianming snorted coldly. He took two steps back to stand in front of Ji Yuan.

“Mr. Ji, do you think we should break into the Netherworld Ghost Palace directly and bring Old Ghost Wuya out?”

Ji Yuan looked at this mansion brimming with ghostly energy, then at the overwhelming eerie, ghostly aura surrounding the city center. He feared that if he turned against them now, countless ghosts would immediately descend. Being too aggressive would be counterproductive, so he shook his head.

“Forget it. It is his celebration, after all. Since he promised to release him, there’s no need to make a big fuss.”

“Alright, I understand!”

Gao Tianming replied respectfully, before turning back to face the ghost guards once more.

“Very well, I will wait for Old Ghost Wuya to release them tonight. However, you should also tell Old Ghost Wuya not to blame me for giving him a warning. If anything were to happen to one of my friends, the consequences would be far more serious than he imagined! Take us to our courtyard to rest.”

“Yes, sir, this way please!”

Once the procession from Tianshui Lake had left, another ghost soldier entered the mansion and quietly reported to the City Lord. They had noticed Gao Tianming’s respect for Ji Yuan, and found it somewhat unusual.

Deep within the palace, inside the ghostly residence, Xin Wuya frowned in thought as he heard the ghost soldier’s second report.

“Go and find Xiang Zhong. If there are mortals there, bring them here.”

“Yes! As the City Lord commands!”

. . .

The sky gradually darkened, and the lights in the city began to light up by themselves. While people in the living Yang world would now be returning home, the Endless Ghost City grew more and more lively, as if the entire city had come alive.4

The Netherworld Ghost Palace had already sent ghostly servants to invite Gao Tianming over to the banquet, and everyone was preparing to depart for the feast.

Only now did Ji Yuan truly grasp the depths of this ghost town. Standing in the courtyard, he gazed out at the city. An endless ghostly aura covered the sky, practically obscuring the sun. The streets and alleyways bustled with activity. There were probably at least hundreds of thousands of ghosts within the city, and countless among them were already quite powerful.

It was clear that what he had seen outside the city last night had been obscured by some kind of barrier.

“I didn’t expect the scale of this ghost city to be so astonishing!”

Gao Tianming frowned to the side.

“Indeed. I underestimated that Old Ghost Wuya. Endless Ghost City has been established in the wilderness for centuries. It has developed from its starting point as a barren graveyard, quietly growing into its present state.”

Niu Batian glanced at Yan Fei, who clutched his sword with a solemn expression, then also gazed outside.

“This is the first time that this Old Bull has seen so many ghosts. Mr. Ji, do you think that all the deceased in the country of Zuyue have ended up here?”

Ji Yuan sighed.

“That’s not the case. There are probably far more than two hundred thousand people who die in the country of Zuyue each year”

“So many?”

The Old Bullw as a little dazed.

“And that is the lowest estimate!”

Ji Yuan was not joking, nor exaggerating. Although he didn’t have statistics, even with the social environment and medical conditions of his country in his previous life, millions of people died each year. The country of Zuyue, despite having a significantly smaller population, had poor living conditions and abysmal medical care, so hundreds of thousands of deaths per year was no exaggeration.

“Mr. Ji, Brother Niu, Brother Yan, let’s go. We can now go find the Lord Old Ghost Wuya.”

The group set off together. Soon, the ghost servants guided them to the Nether Ghost Mansion.

The banquet was a grand affair, with tables and benches set up in a rectangular open space within the mansion. In addition to the numerous ghosts and familiar monsters of Endless Ghost City, there were also many divine path practitioners, including mountain gods, land gods, river gods, even ghosts and underworld gods like city gods.

Laughter, roars, and even the sounds of sobbing filled the room. The crowd was bustling with activity.

By the time Gao Tianming, Ji Yuan, and the others arrived, numerous guests had already taken their seats. Ji Yuan’s furrowed brows had yet to relax the entire time he walked.

In Dazhen, even the thought of seeing so many ghosts and gods sitting together with so many demons and evil spirits would be unbelievable, let alone actually seeing this sight.

Regardless of how much this scene consisted of hypocrisy or slander, or of how many of the attendees were vicious or perverse, Ji Yuan widened his eyes to observe his surroundings. He could see a mingling of various auras, many of which were far from pure or righteous. The scene made him feel as though he were inhaling smog or miasma the entire time he walked.

‘When humanity declines, evil flourishes; when the world is in chaos, evil rises unchecked. This is precisely what is happening here! ‘

This thought encapsulated Ji Yuan’s feelings about the country of Zuyue.

As Ji Yuan was pondering, they had already reached Gao Tianming’s seat, positioned not too far away from the main host’s seat.

“Mr. Ji, please take a seat!”

Gao Tianming invited Ji Yuan to sit down, and then sat down with Xia Qiu, Niu Batian and Yan Fei.

As more and more guests took their seats, the surrounding zithers, pipa, suona, gongs, and drums began to play, creating a strange and eerie style of music. Ji Yuan could only say that it was very appropriate for the occasion.

T/n: On a completely unrelated note, I’ve started listening to “Welcome to Night Vale.” It’s flipping AWESOME, I have been cracking up at each and every episode, and I generally don’t like horror at all. Please don’t spoil me! 😀

幽冥 (yōumíng): hell/netherworld — very explicitly not the underworld, 鬼 (guǐ): ghost, 府 (fǔ): seat of government/official residence/mansion. The last character was translated as “palace” despite the strong connotation of “official/governmental residence” because that would have been a very awkward translation. This also fits with how I’ve been using “City Lord” instead of “mayor” or “County Magistrate”

Previously translated as “Wuya City,” like the name of the City Lord. His name actually translates to 无 (wú): to lack, 涯 (yá): border/horizon, with 鬼城 (guǐchéng) being “ghost town,” hence “Endless Ghost City.” I have gone back to previous chapters and changed the city name there as well.

i.e. census

T/n: LOL the puns

Chapter 309: These Guys Are Seeking Death

Gao Tianming and Niu Batian clearly noticed Mr. Ji’s displeasure. The deeper his unhappiness grew, the straighter the two monsters sat, as if emphasizing the difference between themselves and the other demons around them. However, in the process, their gazes betrayed a slight gloating.

After Ji Yuan and the others took their seats, the dishes were quickly served, without even waiting for the City Lord to arrive.

Slightly different from what they might have imagined, most of the dishes were steaming hot, and the drinks were also largely intoxicating, in stark contrast to many other places in the city.

Clearly, the Netherworld Ghost Palace had put considerable effort into this celebration banquet.

Ji Yuan glanced at the dishes, picked up his chopsticks, and had a taste. While the flavor wasn’t as good as many of the delicacies he had tried before, it was still passable.

Seeing Ji Yuan pick up his chopsticks, Niu Batian, who had long since been struggling to resist, quickly picked up his own chopsticks and started eating, inviting Yan Fei to join him. Gao Tianming picked up the wine jug and refilled Ji Yuan’s glass.

“Mr. Ji, what do you think of this Ghost City celebration banquet?”

Ji Yuan looked at him, picked up the glass, took a sip of the wine, and said in a low voice.

“Nothing could be worse than this foul atmosphere!”

The current situation, to some extent, was a supernatural reflection of the collapse of the country of Zuyue. Moreover, in a time when the world was filled with foul and evil spirits, the monsters that flourished would most often be malicious.

However, the current state was a part of the general trend and evolution of the world. While the chivalrous and righteous people of the world, along with the immortal cultivators, could eliminate evil and slay demons, in the grand scheme of things, they had little decisive influence on the chaos currently breeding in Zuyue.

Human nature was complex, and the world continuously underwent complex changes. Interfering too deeply would not only cause unnecessary trouble, but might also fail to achieve the desired results. This had been proven countless times throughout history. It was best to let humanity evolve on its own, and nudge the situation at the opportune moment.

However, at such moments, some cultivators in the mortal realm would inevitably become too deeply entangled with the fate of humanity, driven by either an unclear greater good or by personal gain, often resulting in unsatisfactory outcomes.

Ji Yuan’s thoughts were obviously drifting away at this moment. Gao Tianming saw that he was holding his wine glass, deep in thought, but he didn’t dare to disturb him, assuming that Mr. Ji was in a bad mood.

Just then, a loud, echoing voice approached, filled with laughter.

“Hahahaha… I didn’t expect Master Gao of Tianshui Lake would also come and honor us with a visit to the Netherworld Palace of the Endless Ghost City. Come, come, Master Gao, let me toast to you! After the banquet here is over, please come to my place again to have a good meal. I’ll get you a pair of virgins, Master Gao, no matter how many times I eat them, I never tire of the flavor, hahahahaha…”

A demonic creature, radiating a ferocious and menacing aura, approached Gao Tianming with a wine jug and a wine glass, laughing loudly and enthusiastically.

Even though Gao Tianming was a flood dragon who could control water, at this moment, cold sweat broke out on his forehead. Out of the corner of his eye, he could sense Mr. Ji’s cold gaze on him.

“Bang–!”

Gao Tianming slammed his palm on the table and stood up angrily, staring at the newcomer with an icy gold glare.

“Who are you? When did I become so familiar with you? Do you truly still eat virgins?”

Sharp claws flashed from within Gao Tianming’s sleeves. In a blur, he had clamped a hand around the opponent’s neck, dragging him closer. The newcomer was much larger than Gao Tianming, but their relative strength seemed to be inversely proportional to their size.

“Crack… crack…”

The bones and muscles in the monster’s neck protested, and blood even welled up from the skin.

“Ahh-haaah…Master… Master Gao… I… I don’t know how I offended you, Master Gao… I… I apologize… haah… haah…”

At this moment, Ji Yuan’s soft voice rang out.

“There are rules for how to act. Just don’t go against your conscience or morality. There is no need to deliberately prove it to me.”

Gao Tianming heaved a sigh of relief, but still couldn’t hide his anger. He tossed the demon around, tearing off a piece of flesh from his neck. Xia Qiu beside him, having stood up at some point, waved her sleeve as her husband threw the demon man to the side.

“Slap…”

There was a crisp sound, and the demon fell back six or seven zhang, hitting another table with a bang.

The monsters in front of the table just backed away hastily, but they didn’t dare to glare in the direction of Gao Tianming and his wife.

“You went a little too far, but well done!”

Ji Yuan gave a light compliment, which made Gao Tianming so happy that he forced himself not to smile, and spoke in a solemn tone.

“I am just furious at this evil creature for defiling my sight and hearing. If we were in Tianshui Lake, he would be dead and used as fish food!”

The surrounding monsters and ghosts all turned their gazes and looked in their direction, but Gao Tianming’s fierce monstrous aura was overhwhelming. Even if anyone was familiar with the demon who had been beaten, no one would stand up for him. The whole venue inevitably fell silent.

Gao Tianming sneered at the demon struggling to get up, then bowed halfheartedly with cupped hands to the beings around him.

“I am merely in a bad mood. This creature came here looking for trouble, disturbing everyone’s fun. Please forgive me. Let us eat and drink!”

Hearing Gao Tianming’s words, everyone around him, particularly those seated nearby, didn’t dare to say anything more, and continued to eat and drink.

Beside Yan Fei, Niu Batian looked at the demon who, after being beaten, only looked upon them with hatred, but didn’t dare to even utter a harsh word. He whispered to Yan Fei.

“Brother Yan, this Gao Tianming is also a fierce monster! What a shame this Old Bull didn’t get to throw a punch…”

The corner of Yan Fei’s mouth twitched, and he sipped a little more wine, completely ignoring the Old Bull’s sentiments.

Gao Tianming didn’t care about the other guests’ reactions; as long as Mr. Ji had a good impression of him, that was all that mattered. However, he had barely sat down when a loud and indifferent voice came from above.

“Master Gao of Tianshui Lake has a big temper, and he has quite the presence as well!”

The voice came from behind main privacy screen. The Lord of the Endless Ghost City had arrived.

Ji Yuan also looked up. The City Lord of the Endless Ghost City walked out from behind the screen, draped in a black robe and wearing a small crown.1 Although he didn’t have the appearance of a ghost, a wisp of black aura lingered around him, with phantom ghostly figures emerging from within it, giving him a formidable presence.

However, under the scrutiny of Ji Yuan’s widened supernatural eyes, the aura seemed a bit abrupt. While it was indeed a ghostly aura, it would be better if it could return to a natural state.2

Seeing Xin Wuya emerge, Gao Tian stood up again and offered a slgiht bow.

“City Lord Xin, today is your celebration. First, I would like to congratulate you on your great progress in cultivation. However, we have business to discuss. What about the promise you made me earlier this day?”

“Of course I haven’t forgotten!”

As he spoke, Xin Wuya had already seated himself on the main seat. He pointed to one side and spoke to the beings behind him.

“Bring them out.”

As his words fell, several ghost soldiers led four pale people from the back room, and walked around the screen to the host’s seat.

These four individuals were none other than the three siblings of the Ke family and Zhou Xing, who had been captured earlier. They were so frightened that they trembled as they walked, especially now that they had encountered so many evil spirits and monsters along the way. Although they had been informed that they would be released, they still had a horrible feeling of impending death.

The four were brought directly to Xin Wuya, who glanced at the four living people before looking at Gao Tianming.

“Brother Gao, are these four your friends?”

“Who are you informally calling Brother Gao?”

Gao Tianming snorted coldly, then bent slightly to get closer to Ji Yuan.

“Mr. Ji, are these four the ones you were looking for?”

Ji Yuan glanced up.

“I don’t recognize them either, so I can’t tell if there are any missing.”

“That’s easy to resolve.”

Gao Tianming stood up again and asked Zhou Xing and the others.

“You were the ones who drove the carriage into the Eternal Ghost City last night, right? Was there anyone else in the carriage besides you?”

Zhou Xing and the others glanced at each other nervously, until Ke Yundong calmed down and spoke.

“Yes, it was us. There were only the four of us in the carriage!”

“Hahaha…That’s right, Xin Wuya, they are my friends, leave them to me!”

Even Ji Yuan found this a little absurd upon hearing his words. The surrounding guests were even more baffled.

Xin Wuya, sitting in the main host’s seat, narrowed his eyes at Gao Tianming, and reached out a hand to stop the ghost soldier who was about to release them.

“Gao Tianming! Didn’t you say they were your friends?

“Haha……”

Xin Wuya laughed coldly. He didn’t care what happened to these living people, but that didn’t mean he would tolerate being made a fool.

“Since they are your friends, who don’t you recognize them, let alone know how many of them are there?”

Gao Tianming nodded, bowed with cupped hands once more, and apologized.

“It is true, I was being rude. I actually don’t know them, but hearing their plight, I wanted to save them, so I casually claimed to be a friend. I was just afraid that you or the other the hungry ghosts in this ghost city would eat them. Please pass them to me.”

Seeing Gao Tianming actually spoke humbly and even apologized, Xin Wuya felt a pang of relief. However, it was nonetheless absurd for a flood dragon of Tianshui Lake to be so kind as to inexplicably care about the lives of a few mortals. There had to be some other reason.

It certainly wasn’t for the sake of acquiring people to eat, and these four probably did not have any special backgrounds. Recalling the report from the day before, Xin Wuya’s gaze swept over Yan Fei, Niu Batian, and Ji Yuan, before finally settling on the latter. he pointed in his direction.

“Brother Gao, could you please tell me who this gentleman is?”

Just as Gao Tianming was about to speak, Ji Yuan raised his hand to stop him. Gao Tianming fell silent and stood by.

It seemed that others had noticed Ji Yuan as well, and Gao Tianming’s respectful display just now sparked curiosity among the surrounding guests.

Ji Yuan stood up and bowed with cupped hands towards Xin Wuya as gesture of formality.

“I am Ji Yuan, a mere passer-by. Congratulations, City Lord Xin, for your success in cultivation. It was my intent to save these people. Master Gao was simply offering his assistance out of virtue. Might the City Lord release them?”

Xin Wuya’s eyes, shrouded in a ghostly energy, shone with a dark light. He used his ghostly eyes to observe Ji Yuan closely. He sensed no power nor divine light, and his aura appeared perfectly ordinary. He was to all appearances an ordinary mortal. However, his demeanor and composure were beyond the reach of ordinary people, not to mention how Gao Tianming acted almost like a junior or servant by his side.

‘A passer-by? I’m afraid he is more likely an immrotal!’

After a moment of silence, Xin Wuya gave the order.

“Release them!”

The ghost soldier led the group down, only letting go when they had reached Gao Tianming and his group.

The four were still trembling in fear at this moment, completely unsure of their fate. It was Yan Fei who stepped forward to comfort them.

“Don’t panic, everyone. I am Yan Fei, these experts and I are here to rescue you.”

“Yan… Yan Fei? Like the Flying Swordsman, the Great Hero Yan Fei?”

Zhou Xing looked at Yan Fei, then at his attire and sword. He already knew in his heart that this was Yan Fei himself. People tended to trust more in hope in desperate situations, so Zhou Xing was more willing it was truly him.

“That’s me!”

“Amazing, amazing, Brother Ke, ladies, we are saved! This is Hero Yan! It’s Hero Yan himself!”

“Wonderful, I’ve heard about the Flying Swordsman too!”

Ke Yunqin exclaimed excitedly. The other two were delighted as well.

“Please take a seat, everyone. We’ll take care of the rest. We’ll definitely get you out of here!”

“Yes, yes!”

“Yes, we’ll listen to Hero Yan!”

Ji Yuan glanced at the four of them and Yan Fei out of the corner of his eye, smiled, and bowed again to Xin Wuya.

“Thank you, City Lord Xin, for your generosity.”

“Hahaha…”

Xin Wuya simply scoffed, neither returning the gesture, nor saying anything more.

Seemingly seeing that Xin Wuya was in a bad mood, the nearby demons and ghosts flattered him.

“They’re just mortals, after all. If the City Lord craves something, we can always grab some fresh ones for you at any time!”

“Hahaha… That’s right, Lord Gao of Tianshui Lake is so powerful, so we won’t offend him. After today is over, we’ll have a banquet only for the newcomers. Wouldn’t it be more enjoyable then?”

“Hahahaha… wonderful, wonderful…”

“Hmph, well said, hehehe…”

“Ooh, hehehe…”

Now that Xin Wuya had arrived, which emboldened the crowd, the surroundings suddenly echoed with sharp, sarcastic laughter from all around. Although Gao Tianming was a powerful flood dragon, he was only one being in the end, and was now surrounded by many equally powerful ghosts and demons.

More fierce demons and ghosts joined in the boisterous laugher, some even beginning to salivate at the scene of the feast being described, while most of the ghosts and gods present remained silent.

Ji Yuan simply glanced at Xin Wuya, who was sitting in the main seat. Niu Batian, Gao Tianming, Yan Fei, and the four rescued people could already hear a slight ringing in their ears, and a chill ran down their spines.

‘These guys are courting death! ‘

Besides the four people who had just been rescued, everyone around Ji Yuan had this same thought in their hearts.

Chapter 310: Almost Erased

Ji Yuan was naturally extremely disgusted by the uproar around him. However, he knew that he really couldn’t get rid of so many ghosts and monsters at once, particularly in such a stronghold of ghosts and evil spirits like the Endless Ghost City.

But this didn’t mean that Ji Yuan was afraid. In fact, he knew very well that no one, not even Xin Wuya, the City Lord of the Endless Ghost City, could withstand the full power of the Green Vine Sword.

There was no way for him to overwhelmingly control the whole situation, so he would have to make an example of them, to give a warning to the ghosts and demons.1 Ji Yuan had reason to believe that most of the crowd were weaklings,2 and that those few powerful ghosts and monsters were only looking out for themselves. At least, not many would give their lives for Xin Wuya.

As long as he could dispatch Xin Wuya cleanly with a single strike, the vast majority of these troublemakers would most likely not dare to cause any major disturbances. Even if some refused to obey, he also had a readied lightning control technique.

However, Ji Yuan narrowed his eyes and scanned the surroundings, observing the ghostly aura permeating the city. Without Xin Wuya’s restraint, as the City Lord of the Endless Ghost City, the countless ancient ghosts within might not remain there peacefully.

“Hahahahaha…Gao Tianming, although you may be the black dragon of Tianshui Lake, this is still the Endless Ghost City, after all, and only City Lord Xin has the final say!”

“Hehehehe… The guest should follow the wishes of the host. Even if the City Lord changes the banquet now, Master Gao wouldn’t have any objections, right?”

Endless laughter and ridicule filled the air. Gao Tianming’s silence was taken as a sign of weakness, emboldening the demons and evil spirits even further.

Under these circumstances, even the more powerful ghosts and monsters nearby began to take the current situation a bit more seriously.

Some of the spirits and gods could not longer bear to watch, snorting coldly and casting their gazes around, somewhat intimidating the weaker ghosts and monsters.

As the center of the dispute, Ji Yuan, Gao Tianming, and the others naturally attracted the most attention, even City Lord Xin Wuya, who still had yet to react, was watching them.

Xin Wuya suddenly noticed that, aside from the four rescued mortals huddled together and trembling, Gao Tianming, Niu Batian, and the others simply looked around with a cold gaze, showing no trace of anger at all, even boasting sneers tinged with sarcasm.

Ji Yuan, on the other hand, completely ignored everyone around him, simply staring at him, the City Lord. His pale eyes were devoid of emotion — or perhaps seemed to absorb any thoughts or feelings.

For some reason, Xin Wuya felt a strange sense of intimidation at the sight of thoat pair of eyes. They were too peaceful, without any traces of fear, regardless of whether he was bluffing.

Ji Yuan looked at Xin Wuya for a while, then shook out his sleeves and stood up slowly.

“City Lord Xin, I can hardly be considered a guest if I came here uninvited, but I still wish to ask: is this corrupt and filthy atmosphere truly what the City Lord desires?”

Ji Yuan’s words were filled with the sound of an Edict. His voice was still calm and quiet, yet it could be heard by every ghost, monster, and creature present.

Although the sound was not loud at all, it instantly drowned out the laughter and mocking voices in the venue, bringing the hall to an eerie silence.

Everyone’s eyes lingered on Ji Yuan and the City Lord.

Seeing that Xin Wuya narrowed his eyes and was about to speak, Ji Yuan raised a hand to stop him, then opened his eyes a little wider and spoke again lightly.

“I hope the City Lord will speak carefully, and not be provoked to rash decisions. Do not speak out of anger, as anything you say I will take seriously.”

As Ji Yuan spoke, the slight ringing sound faded from the ears of Niu Batian, Gao Tianming, and his wife, but this disappearance brought more chills to their bodies.

The Old Bull, Gao Tianming, and the others all exchanged glances, seemingly able to sense something in each other’s eyes.

High in the sky, the Green Vine Sword continued to rise up to the clouds. A faint symbol, gleaming with a wisp of lightning, emerged from the Green Vine Sword and ascended into the dark cloudy sky.

Bzzzt…Bzzzt…

The entire dark cloud stretched for tens of li. Originally formed through Yin Qi, it now shimmered with faint flashes of lightning. A mantra composed of golden light flickered within the clouds.

If someone were to soar above the clouds at this moment and can see the talisman amidst the fog, they would have seen four characters within the golden light: “Exorcise and Bind Evil.”

Ji Yuan’s lightning control was quite poor, practically the worst of his techniques, but it was nonetheless quite powerful.

This thunder talisman had been nurtured by the previous mysterious yellow Qi, and had absorbed nearly all the water and thunder essence that the Black Dragon Mo had dispersed before his death. After having used it only briefly to dispel the curse from the Black Dragon, Ji Yuan had nurtured it ever since.

Thunder was the most potent technique against Yin creatures and evil. With enough time, Ji Yuan could wait for the thunder talisman to assimilate with the entire cloud. If it dissipated in an instant and transformed into a thunder technique, the force released would unleash a devastating thunderstorm.

As a flood dragon, Gao Tianming was extremely sensitive to the aura of water and thunder. At this moment, besides the sharp, cold feeling he had previously sensed, he now noticed that his hair had begun to flutter lightly, and a tingling sensation rose within his spirit.

He suddenly raised his head and looked at the sky. Although it was still overcast, and the Yin energy still permeated the atmosphere, it seemed as though above the clouds a surge of thunder, a radiant transformation of Yin into Yang, was raging.

‘Who is wielding thunder energy? Such a terrifying and subtle pressure…Mr. Ji! ‘

Gao Tianming gulped hard, feeling out of breath. Coincidentally, his wife also happened to be looking at him with an inexplicable expression. He nodded slightly, and spoke to Xia Qiu with silent transmission.

“Old Ghost Wuya, it’s time for you to make a life-or-death decision!”

Xin Wuya really wanted to sneer at first, but Ji Yuan’s last words inexplicably gave him a sense of alarm. He glanced at Gao Tianming and Niu Batian, and suddenly noticed that the two of them were acting strange. Gao Tianming had lost his previous aggressive demeanor, and his expression was now tinged with fear and horror, though he quickly regained his composure. However, there was no way that this old flood dragon could be afraid of the surrounding ghosts and demons.

Similiarly, Niu Batian, with his simple and honest appearance, had his gaze darting all around, revealing a hint of mockery and gloating.

This kind of micro-expression expressed a kind of desire to watch a good show, but that the people around him were the spotlight. Surely, they weren’t the ones putting on a show, were they?

The sheer intensity of the odd actions made Xin Wuya uneasy. He restrained his ghostly aura somewhat. Instead of directly answering Ji Yuan’s question, he instead gazed out at the surrounding ghosts and demons.

“My Endless Ghost City has never been fond of so-called living sacrifices. Besides, the presence of so many ghosts and gods among the guests is a sign of respect and honor for this City Lord. If you find the food in my Endless Ghost City is not to your liking, you may enjoy your own once you leave the city.”

With this, Xin Wuya suddenly turned to look at Ji Yuan again, his eyes glinting. A chilling ghostly aura erupted, obscuring half of the sky above the palace courtyard.

Whooo… whooo…

A cold wind swept through the space, freezing and frosting over some of the soups and dishes on the nearby tables.

Xin Wuya’s voice, now filled with ghostly echoed, sounded in waves.

“However, should I consider the Immortal’s words just now a threat to me? There are ghosts, demons, gods, and monsters at this banquet. Judging from the sound of the Tao just now, you are the only one here who cultivates immortality. I would like to ask you: if I, Xin Wuya, truly did not see the bigger picture, or if I simply wished to harm you, Immortal, what means would you have to resist?”

But Ji Yuan only really cared about his previous statement. Hearing Xin Wuya’s words, he felt a little relieved. It seemed that conflict could be avoided, and that he could conserve his thunder technique.

“It’s no wonder City Lord Xin can invite many ghosts and gods to congratulate him. The Endless Ghost City truly has its own way of dealing with things. If my words just now offended you, I hope the City Lord can forgive me!”

Ji Yuan didn’t even respond to the implicit threat, nor did he intend to answer Xin Wuya’s subsequent questions. After he bowed with cupped hands and resumed his seat. Simultaneously, he glanced at Niu Batian and Gao Tianming with a warning look, hoping to prevent them from causing any more trouble.

The Old Bull and Gao Tianming’s hearts trembled suddenly, especially the latter, who was still in shock. Thinking back just now, I felt that his behavior had simply been provocative, hoping that the other party would anger Mr. Ji, a combination of complacent and gloating. This was clearly not appreciated by Mr. Ji.

Faced with Ji Yuan’s response, Xin Wuya felt as though he had punched into empty space. Nearby, a ghost general with a sinister look in his eyes approached him, leaning in and muttering.

Gao Tianming glanced at Mr. Ji and, with a sudden thought, he hurriedly kept his gaze down while speaking to Xin Wuya via mental transmission.

“Old Ghost Wuya, stop while you’re ahead!”

Xin Wuya had been listening to the ghost general’s words when Gao Tianming’s voice suddenly came to his ears. He was slightly taken aback. He looked at Gao Tianming, but found that the other party was just sitting there, staring at the table, not facing him.

“Don’t look. It’s me, Old Gao. Old Ghost Wuya, I’m reminding you, you have already escaped with your life, don’t be ungrateful. I’m not threatening you. It’s just that I got carried away just now, and Mr. Ji was displeased. So let me remind you again Mr. Ji is not someone you can afford to offend. The clouds overhead are already surging with thunder. The Endless Ghost City could collapse at any moment. That’s all I can say. It is up to you whether you believe me or not!”

Gao Tianming didn’t even lift his face throughout the entire transmission, but it had a profound impact on Xin Wuya’s judgment.

After squinting at the table, he glanced at the sky from the corner of his eye. He couldn’t see what was happening in the clouds, but he nimbly transformed his own ghost energy into a line, rapidly extending it from his head upwards.

After just a few breaths…

“Bzzzt…”

Xin Wuya’s body went slightly numb, and his heart stuttered. He turned to look at the ghost general who had been spouting insults at Ji Yuan and the others.

“Are you finished?”

“City Lord, we are so far away from Tianshui Lake, and that immortal cultivator…”

“Get lost!”

As soon as Xin Wuya revealed his palpable fury, the ghost general didn’t dare to say anything more and hurriedly retreated to the side.

“As expected of a brilliant Immortal Cultivator, you are truly magnanimous. Mr. Ji as an Immortal Cultivator has honored us by attending this ceremony, and I am not one to disregard the situation. Let us simply treat previous events as a misunderstanding!”

WIth this, Xin Wuya returned to normal, brushign aside the previous incident, and invited the guests to resume the feast.

No one, neither host nor guests, disrupted the atmosphere. The music of the suona and zither resumed. After a brief moment of tension, the whole celebration banquet quickly returned to the previous lively and bustling atmosphere.

Rather than a celebration, it was more apt to say that it was an announcement of the City Lord of the Endless Ghost City’s remarkable advancement in cultivation, and as such a demonstration of the Endless Ghost City’s influence on all surrounding ghosts, monsters, and gods.

During this period, some interesting developments occurred. Most of the evil spirits ignored Gao Tianming’s general location, and aside from some friendly monsters who had a good relationship with Tianshui Lake who came to pay him a visit, most monsters at least pretended to distance themselves from him. However, many ghosts and gods came to toast Gao Tianming.

The fervor of the banquet did not subside until after the Yin hour,3 when Ji Yuan and his group departed.

The convoy from Tianshui Lake did not stay overnight either. After the banquet, they passed through the bustling streets of the Endless Ghost City, before exiting through the city gates as soon as the banquet had ended.

Outside, the bustle of the Endless Ghost City gradually subsided, growing increasingly further away before fading completely. Yan Fei and the other four mortals in the group breathed a sigh of relief. It was clearly much warmer out here than in the city.

At this point, Niu Batian finally couldn’t hold back his words.

“Mr. Ji, are you just going to leave this ghost city alone?”

“Oh? What do you think I should do?”

“With your supernatural powers, I don’t believe it’s outside of your power to wipe out the ghost city entirely!”

Niu Batian hesitated before speaking. Gao Tianming and his wife were also listening nearby. Ji Yuan just smiled and shook his head. He did indeed have that ability, but he would feel a little distressed for some time; it would be best if he could avoid it.

Gao Tianming hearing those words, he thought to himself:

‘Perhaps it was indeed almost wiped out…’

Before Ji Yuan could reply, Lady Gao, Xia Qiu, said thoughtfully.

“Uncle Niu does not appreciate Mr. Ji’s painstaking efforts. While destroying the Endless Ghost City might not be a difficult undertaking for Mr. Ji, there are countless evil spirits in the city, and it is not certain that they would all be eradicated. Once the Endless Ghost City is destroyed, there would be one less place to contain and restrain evil spirits. The country of Zuyue and the northern lands are all in chaos, a breeding ground of evil spirits. In the future, there will be wars and chaos under the sun in the Yang world, and evil spirits rising in the nighttime Yin world. With the rise of corrupt officials and bandits, the power of the gods will be weakened, and the world will suffer desolation!”

“Wife, you are absolutely right. I, Gao, also came to this realization at the banquet. Brother Niu, do you understand now?”

Gao Tianming gave Xia Qiu an approving look, while patting Niu Batian on the shoulder, speaking in an earnest tone.

Ji Yuan looked at Xia Qiu and nodded.

“Lady Gao is quite insightful. Additionally, City Lord Xin is can be regarded as a great talent among ghost cultivators.”

As Ji Yuan spoke, he gazed toward the road ahead. Ghostly figures appeared there one after another, Xin Wuya standing prominently among them. Beside him stood a ghostly deity in a blue robe and a tall hairpin crown.

Seeing the convoy approaching, the ghost deity was the first to bow and greet them.

“The City God of Queliu City, Taoist Yue Jing, greets Mr. Ji!

The exact phrasing used is “kill the chicken to scare the monkey — or rather, kill the ghosts to warn the demons.” The idiom means “make an example out of someone to threaten others,” and JY follows it up with a bit of humor about the ghosts being the chicken (weaklings) and the demons being the monkey (the main threat)

3-5 am

Chapter 311: The Edict and the Lightning Talisman

The Tianshui Lake contingent naturally halted. Ji Yuan and the others slowly walked to the front of the group, looking towards the standing ghostly figure and the City God.

The divine light of this City God’s power was far inferior to that of the City Gods of some of the larger cities in Dazhen. At most, it was in par with the underworld deities of some of the mid-sized to larger counties. However, given the current state of collapse of the country of Zuyue’s divinities, his cultivation could be considered quite outstanding.

Even with the current state of affairs, the City God was still a city god, after all. Even when attending the Endless Ghost City’s celebration, he merely offered Xin Wuya a few congratulatory words. He didn’t pay any grand respect to monsters like Gao Tianming or Niu Batian, nor did he do more than glance at mortals like Yan Fei. He reserved his deference solely for Ji Yuan.

Only after paying his respect to Ji Yuan did the City God straighten up and bow slightly to Gao Tianming and the others.

“Greetings, Lord Gao, Lady Gao, and esteemed guests!”

“Greetings, Lord City God! “

Ji Yuan returned the greeting with a brief bow. Gao Tianming and his wife, along with Niu Batian, didn’t dare to be negligent. It was a rare occasion for them to be able to greet an underworld deity and address them directly as “Lord City God.”

Then, everyone’s gaze followed Ji Yuan’s, and they turned to the the City Lord of the Endless Ghost City, Xin Wuya.

“Hey, Old Ghost Wuya, why are you blocking us here? “

Gao Tianming chuckled mockingly, though in his heart he could somewhat guess his purpose in coming here.

However, it was clear that Xin Wuya, as the City Lord of the Endless Ghost City, still retained some pride. Unlike the City God of Queliu City, who was reverential, he simply offered a bow with cupped hands to Ji Yuan and his companions, offering no words of respect.

“Mr. Ji, Brother Gao previously sent a spiritual transmission, saying that you spared my life, and that my Endless Ghost City was in danger of collapse. It is not that I disbelieve Brother Gao’s words, but there is one matter that remains unclear: if you truly possessed such power, sir, why then did you endure the mockery of ghosts and demons during the celebration, and why would you spare my Endless Ghost City?”

Although Xin Wuya’s words weren’t exactly an accusation, they certainly weren’t polite. Thinking back, if it had been him in Ji Yuan’s position, Xin Wuya would certainly not have held back if he could easily destroy his opponent, so his suspicions only grew stronger.

As for the City God of Queliu City, Xin Wuya had merely encountered him by chance outside the city. The former had also been waiting for Ji Yuan by the road, so the two simply waited together. However, out of courtesy, the City God had taken the initiative, and was now content to let Xin Wuya speak first.

Hearing Xin Wuya’s words, Gao Tianming curled his lips in a fleeting sneer.

‘Just an old ghost who hasn’t experienced much in the world. Just because others afford you some respect, you think you rule the Underworld? How could a country bumpkin like you comprehend the thoughts and intentions of a true master? You only see the immediate consequences of life and death in front of you, while others have already seen through the thoughts of all sentient beings.’

Alright, Gao Tianming admitted that even he himself had come here to show respect, but that didn’t mean that he truly respected Xin Wuya. It was merely about maintaining a basic superficial relationship, as they both lived in the country of Zuyue and would inevitably cross paths.

Although Gao Tianming’s facial expression was fleeting, truthfully, he hadn’t hidden it much, so both Xin Wuya and the City God caught that instant. The former felt a flicker of hidden anger, while the latter’s expression grew increasingly thoughtful.

Ji Yuan, for his part, calmly observed Xin Wuya from head to toe, but didn’t speak immediately. His supernatural eyes were fully open at close range, taking in the aura of this ancient ghost cultivator. Facing those pale eyes, Xin Wuya felt as though he no longer had any secrets left to himself.

“Xin Wuya, I was waiting for you to seek me out. “

Ji Yuan’s voice was indifferent, yet solemn. He didn’t address Xin Wuya respectfully with ‘City Lord’ as he had before before, but instead called him by his full name.

“The divine path in the country of Zuyue has declined, and many places have become havens for malevolent spirits and demons. You established the Endless Ghost City, taking in numerous wandering ghosts, fierce specters, and even vengeful spirits. You only allow entry, but no exit. While this was only done for your own cultivation, it is a good rule. “

At this point, Ji Yuan looked back at the Endless Ghost City in the distance, where only the glow of city lights could be seen on the horizon, its outline no longer visible. He continued.

“And, previously, the four distressed travelers were indeed recovered from the city, which means you kept your word. They did not cause trouble in the city, which shows you have a degree of restraint. Furthermore, cultivating to your current level as a ghost is truly remarkable. “

“Oh? Hahahaha. . . So, the Master has a soft spot for talent? How rare! “

Xin Wuya spoke sarcastically, when he suddenly noticed Gao Tianming to the side had just suppressed the hint of surprise on his face. He had clearly extremely astonished by Ji Yuan’s words.

“Indeed, I do have a slight appreciation for talent. Dynasties rise and fall; this is the universal cyclical trend of the world, and I will not impede it. However, it would be best if the chaos and calamity suffered by sentient beings could be lessened. As the saying goes, ‘in troubled times, use harsh measures; for chronic illnesses, administer potent medicine.’1 The existence of the Endless Ghost City is a fortuitous unintentional opportunity. Given the underworld’s insufficient control, it can help control the demons and evil spirits that infest a vast area of Zuyue’s southern regions.”

Ji Yuan’s words were extremely blunt, almost explicitly telling Xin Wuya, “I, Ji Yuan, merely find you barely acceptable, and you and your Endless Ghost City are still useful, which is why I didn’t act.”

“Hahaha…”

Ji Yuan chuckled faintly.

“Whether you feel indignant or harbor doubts, it is fate that you have come here to meet me.”

As Ji Yuan spoke, he opened his right hand, palm facing upwards, and pointed towards the distance in the direction of the Endless Ghost City from which they had emerged.

His gesture made everyone present, from Xin Wuya to the small water spirits in the convoy, to instinctively turn towards the Endless Ghost City. A few breaths later, the clouds above the ghost city began to undergo mysterious transformations.

While the lower layer remained thickly shrouded in dark clouds, pressing down like a lid on the city below, the upper layer revealed streaks of golden lightning.

“Crack…”

“Crackle…”

“Boom…”

“Rumble…”

The sound of thunder echoed, as if the clouds were exploding. Even as far as they were here, many spirits were so terrified they covered their ears in fear.

The distant lightning was so intense that it seemed to transform the lower layer of clouds above the Endless Ghost City into one golden cloud with swirling arcs, like a golden canopy covering the vast area both inside and outside the ghost city.

“Rumble…”

Amidst the thunder, the golden canopy’s light grew increasingly brilliant, yet its range shrank, until only a single golden point of light remained above the entire cloud layer. Then, that light rapidly flew towards them at a speed visible to the naked eye.

After only six or seven breaths, a golden talisman, gleaming with mystical lightning, flew into Ji Yuan’s palm. The talisman lacked both paper or jade as its base: it was merely formed from a radiant, luminous text woven from the void. Upon it were the four characters: “Exorcise and Bind Evil.”

It was this very incantation that represented the essence of the Black Flood Dragon’s life force, water essence, and lightning energy, transformed in its dying moment. Furthermore, it contained the mysterious power of Ji Yuan’s Edict, elevating it to a new level. Within the talisman lay the boundless might of Celestial Thunder.

Faint rumbles of thunder emerged, even creating an illusion of lightning shifting the air, as if Ji Yuan were cradling a mighty bolt in his palm.

The lightning from the talisman talisman brightly illuminated the surrounding road, casting the faces of the bystanders in a deathly pale light. Many water spirits instinctively shrank behind the carriage, not daring to look closely.

The golden talisman script lingered in Ji Yuan’s palm for two breaths, before naturally flying into his sleeve. All sounds, colors, and radiance vanished, drawn into his sleeve along with the talisman.

“Xin Wuya, you ask why I spared you and your Endless Ghost City? That question was actually meaningless. Of course, now, now I consider you spared.”

Ghosts didn’t sweat, but at this moment, Xin Wuya truly felt a cold sweat pouring down his face, his thoughts somewhat jumbled.

‘In other words, if I hadn’t come…’

He understood the meaning of the golden characters that had appeared just now, but the immense power they contained remained unclear to him. Still, from just that momentary glimpse of their radiance, it wasn’t hard to grasp how powerful they were. This Mr. Ji’s seemingly light words carried an immense weight!

The Old Bull wiped a bead of sweat from his face and stealthily approached Gao Tianming, speaking in a faint whisper.

“Old Gao… Master Ji doesn’t seem as easygoing as he appears…”

“Yes, indeed…”

Gao Tianming was arguably the only person among all present, apart from Ji Yuan, who could most accurately sense the terrifying power of that golden talisman script. That Mr. Ji had truly intended to obliterate the Endless Ghost City.

Seeing his objective achieved, Ji Yuan smiled again.

“You needn’t be nervous. This Lighting Talisman was not easy to refine; I would have been heartbroken if I truly had to use it. Besides, there are likely many benevolent spirits within the ghost city. Even if I controlled this lightning talisman perfectly, it would be difficult to avoid accidental harm. I am relieved that it didn’t come to that.”

‘Accidental harm? More like accidental deaths!’

Given the overwhelming power difference, Xin Wuya no longer dared to be arrogant. He immediately bowed deeply, even more respectfully than the City God had just moments ago.

“Thank you, Master, for sparing my life! “

Gao Tianming, standing nearby, couldn’t help but ask at this moment.

“Master Ji! I would like to ask, what wondrous lightning-control technique did you use just now? Even I, as a flood dragon, felt my skin tingle with fear, as if stung by needles! “

Ji Yuan didn’t hide anything.

“This is an Imperial Edict Lightning Talisman. It is infused with a divine Edict and the power of Celestial Thunder. The vital essence of Celestial Thunder contained within the incantation…”

At this point, Ji Yuan smiled, looking at Gao Tianming.

“It’s roughly the equivalent to the sum of the entire essence of a flood dragon of your cultivation level! Then, it would use that power to command lightning and strike it down in one blow. It’s power should be quite impressive! “

“Hiss…”

Gao Tianming instinctively shuddered at his words, and Xin Wuya’s ghost form trembled as well.

‘A talisman condensed from centuries of flood dragon vital essence, with all its power unleashed in a single strike?’

This wasn’t just simple arithmetic; it was absolutely not something even another flood dragon could withstand!

“City Lord Xin! “

“Present!”

Ji Yuan’s sudden call startled Xin Wuya, who hastily responded.

“I said you did well, but do you understand the deeper meaning behind those words? “

“I-I understand! “

“Good. That is all. We will take our leave.”

After Ji Yuan finished speaking, he glanced at the City God on the other side, then took the lead and strode forward. Niu Batian quickly pulled Yan Fei along to follow.

After Gao Tianming recovered from his shock, he also bowed briefly to Xin Wuya, before leading his wife in pursuit. The aquatic sprits hesitated and trembled for a while before finally settling in their carriages and moving to follow. The four mortals, frightened and mentally drained from excessive terror, had long since fallen asleep in the carriage. Not even the thunder could wake them.

Xin Wuya stood there in a daze, feeling surprisingly as though he had survived a great calamity.

Ji Yuan, already some distance away, observed the newly formed chess piece in his hand, then turned his head to glance behind him. He spoke silently, sending a mental transmission back over the distance.

“City Lord Xin, those evil spirits in the Endless Ghost City who clamored for a feast of living people during the grand celebration—if they serve no crucial purpose, dispose of them.”

Xin Wuya quickly bowed in the direction the carriages had departed, and answered with a loud, “Yes! “

The dozen or so evil spirits and vengeful ghosts who had been clamoring the most fiercely naturally surfaced in Xin Wuya’s mind at that moment. All were marked for death. After a moment’s thought, he decided to have the Dark Minister executed as well.

Chapter 312: Bringing It Up A Level

After the aquatic spirits’ procession had departed, the City God of Queliu City, who had been standing to one side, took the initiative to bow farewell to Xin Wuya.

“City Lord Xin, I shall take my leave now.”

Only after the City God spoke did Xin Wuya remember the presence of the other ghostly deity beside him. He returned the salute and asked with a hint of confusion.

“Lord City God, were you not also waiting here for Mister Ji? He has already left.”

The City God accepted the bow and smiled.

“Indeed, I originally wished to confirm something with Mister Ji, but now that everything has been settled, there’s no need. Ghosts and gods should not leave their own jurisdiction for too long. City Lord Xin, we shall part ways here.”

The City God’s purpose in waiting there was not only to understand the supernatural powers of this mysterious immortal cultivator, but also to gain some understanding of the Endless Ghost City. Besides Xin Wuya, the underworld deities were probably the ones most concerned about the Endless Ghost City. Now that a far better outcome than anticipated had been achieved, there was naturally no need for him to do anything further.

With these words, the City God, who had come alone, turned and departed with a carefree demeanor, transforming into a streak of light and flying away.

Now there truly were no more outsiders left. Xin Wuya glanced at the two ghost generals and several ghost soldiers he had brought with him. These trusted subordinates of his had just been terrified as well. When that lightning talisman had approached, all the ghost soldiers, aside from the ghost generals, only just barely managed to prevent themselves from turning and fleeing. They at least fared much better than those aquatic spirits and monsters.

“Do you remember who those individuals were at the banquet earlier who were making a fuss about wanting to eat a feast of living people?”

The moment Xin Wuya asked, one of the ghost generals beside him replied.

“I remember some of them. As for the rest, even if I don’t recall them clearly, I can make a guess.”

Being able to attend the banquet meant they held some status in the Endless Ghost City. As they lived in the same city, most knew each other well. It wasn’t hard to determine who would be most eager to eat human flesh, and who would leap out at such an opportunity.

“It’s good that you remember. When I return later. . . better to kill a thousand innocents than let a single one escape!”

Xin Wuya narrowed his eyes, gazing back in the direction of the Endless Ghost City,1 and quietly gave his subordinates a summary of Ji Yuan’s intentions. Mr. Ji’s original words were to “dispose of them if they served no crucial purpose,” while in Xin Wuya’s interpretation, it became “slaughter them all.”

As Xin Wuya spoke, the ghost generals and ghost soldiers beside him either frowned or nodded. Once he had roughly outlined his goal, he suddenly smiled.

“All these ancient old ghosts dispersing their Yin energy—what a fortuitous opportunity. I can use this chance to further enhance the Yin energy of my Endless Ghost City. Besides, the golden light of the Lightning Talisman just now was probably seen by all the ghosts in the city. We even have an excuse to act.”

Once Xin Wuya returned to the Endless Ghost City, he could see that many of the ghosts still had expressions terror and lingering fear on their faces. Many had never witnessed lightning above their heads ever since they began to reside in the Endless Ghost City, but the entire sky had transformed into a golden thundercloud just now, giving them a terrifying sense of impending doomsday.

Fortunately, that terrifying aura vanished as swiftly as it had come.

Because of this incident, word quickly spread from the Netherworld Ghost Palace that a powerful master, a friend of the City Lord, congratulated him on refining his Ghostly Yin Body, and aided the Endless Ghost City refine its on Yin energy. Soon, the ghostly Yin energy of the Endless Ghost City would reach new heights.

Upon Xin Wuya’s return, the Endless Ghost City closed all of its gates and activates its barrier techniques, effectively creating a trap with no escape.

Xin Wuya harbored no fear of failure, nor was he particularly concerned about his movements being exposed. Ultimately, the Endless Ghost City remained under his sole authority.

An hour into the hour of Mao,2 in a pavilion deep within the Netherworld Ghost Palace…

City Lord Xin Wuya sat upon a meditation cushion, focusing his mind in quiet cultivation. After returning to the city, he had dispatched many of his trusted subordinates, led by several highly skilled ghost generals and a contingent of ghost soldiers and guards to execute those vicious and malevolent ghosts.

At this moment, Xin Wuya could clearly sense the rising Yin energy in the city, likely due to the purging of so many evil spirits.

At this moment, a ghost general approached the pavilion entrance, bowed with cupped hands, and reported to the inside.

“Reporting to the City Lord, we have slain over two hundred evil spirits, including those we merely suspected to be a part. The Dark Minister has also been apprehended; he begs for an audience with the City Lord.”

Xin Wuya opened his eyes and looked at the ghost general outside.

“What does he have to say?”

“He says he is utterly loyal to the City Lord, and that he captured those mortals only to offer them as tribute to the City Lord. If one were to truly investigate, he claims he actually saved those four mortals, otherwise they would have long been devoured by the ghosts in the Tengyuan Restaurant.”

A faint smile crossed Xin Wuya’s pale face.

“Nothing else?”

The ghost general thought for a moment before confirming.

“No, that’s all.”

“Alright. Trap his soul in refined ghostly soul-binding chains, then execute him.”

“Understood!”

The ghost general replied hesitantly, before looking at the City Lord and asking:

“My Lord, City Lord, you will not meet with him? This subordinate truly believes he is loyal to you.”

“He wishes to see me simply to beg for his life, but he will die whether he sees me or not. By the way, what is Tengyuan Restaurant?”

The ghost general thought for a moment before replying.

“I believe it is a rather well-known restaurant in the city.”

“Very well, eliminate all ghosts connected to that restaurant as well, and relate my answer to Xiang Zhong. You may leave.”

“As you command!”

The ghost general bowed again, then took a few steps back, before turning and leaving.

…

Inside a dark prison cell in the Netherworld Ghost Palace, the Dark Minister watched with anticipation as the outer door was opened, and then as the ghost general walked into his cell, followed by ghost soldiers. He couldn’t help but grasp the dark black-iron prison bars, excitedly demanding the outcome of his request.

“General Wu! General Wu, have you met the City Lord? Has the City Lord agreed to see me? There must be a misunderstanding; I, Xiang Zhong, will personally explain to the City Lord and plead for forgiveness…”

“Xiang Zhong, the City Lord has agreed to see you! Open the door for him. “

“Yes!”

A ghost soldier took out a key that emanated malevolent qi and unlocked the prison door.

“Excellent, excellent!”

The Dark Minister emerged from the cell with a look of relief, his face brimming with gratitude. He bowed deeply to the usually expressionless ghost general, cupping his hands in thanks.

“Thank you for the report, General, thank you. . .”

The instant the Dark Minister finished his bow, he looked up.

“Shiiing…”

The sound of a blade being drawn from its sheath echoed, accompanied by the gleam of a blade. With merely a fleeting afterimage, the Dark Minister’s words of thanks came to an abrupt halt. The ghost’s body had been decapitated. It subsequently transformed into a thick mist that dissipated outside the prison cell.

…

Gao Tianming’s convoy had long since left the Endless Ghost City.

After dawn, some of the aquatic spirits and monsters in the convoy began to reveal parts of their true forms. Numerous fish scales appeared on the faces of some servants, while others developed keratinous growths or even long whiskers.

These spirits and monsters were not powerful beings themselves, let along fully-transformed monsters. They were merely using various illusion techniques. The sun’s power, however, was known to suppress many Yin arts, evil techniques, and illusions. Many evil creatures could only manifest and deceive people at night.

The previous day, when the carriage procession could be seen during the day, it had been hidden within the bounds of the Endless Ghost City, shrouded in a place of Yin energy. Now, outside, illuminated by sunlight, many of these spirits fully revealed their true forms.

The four rescued individuals naturally awoke upon feeling the sunlight streaming into the carriage. Upon spotting the true appearance of the servants around them, they no longer dared to stay in the carriage, insisting instead on walking alongside Yan Fei.

A peculiar mist continuously followed the procession. Though it wasn’t noticeable while walking, it actually allowed them to travel much faster than they would have on foot. Gao Tianming described it as a kind of “Wave-Gliding Technique.”

Around midday, the procession arrived at a river. This was where the Tianshui Lake contingent would enter the water, swimming until they reached the Tianshui Lake.

The carriage and the spirits were already eagerly entering the water, while Gao Tianming and his wife remained standing on the bank.

“Mister Ji, if you have the time, please visit my Tianshui Lake Underwater Palace. While I cannot provide divine brews like the Dragon Ambergris, I do have some fine wines!”

Gao Tianming and his wife stood solemnly by the water’s edge, bowing with cupped hands to Ji Yuan in farewell.

“Indeed, Mister Ji. Whenever you visit, I, Xia Qiu, will personally cook a table full of my specialty dishes for you.”

Ji Yuan briefly pondered what sort of process cooking underwater would entail, while he also bowed with cupped hands in farewell back.

“If I have time, I will certainly visit.”

Xia Qiu immediately beamed in delight upon hearing Ji Yuan’s words, happily responding:

“I will take your words seriously, sir, you mustn’t break your promise!”

“Ah, my dear wife, what are you saying? Mister Ji is a profound Taoist master, how could he possibly go back on his word!”

The couple, echoed each other, their faces blooming with smiles. Not wanting to overshadow this good news, they also bowed and said goodbye to Niu Batian, Yan Fei, and the others.

“Brother Niu, Brother Yan, and the four of you, may we meet again if fate allows. I and my wife shall now enter the water. We part ways here!”

“Goodbye, Brother Gao! “

“Until we meet again!”

Niu Batian and Yan Fei bowed and spoke in return, while the other four merely exchanged polite farewells, not daring utter a word, knowing full well that their hosts had no interest in them.

After bidding farewell to everyone, Gao Tianming and his wife stepped into the water. There was no grand parting of the river, nor were there any whirlpools; they simply let the water rise over them, from their feet to their heads, before only a few faint ripples were left on the surface.

Numb from the repeated shocks, the group of four breathed a sigh of relief. Ke Yundong even spoke with a hint of emotion.

“I never imagined I would witness such a wondrous sight in my lifetime…”

Zhou Xing gazed at Ji Yuan in awe, then turned to Niu Batian and Yan Fei, stepping closer to quietly whisper to them.

“Great Hero Yan, Great Hero Niu, are all the monsters and ghosts gone now?”

Before Yan Fei could speak, Niu Batian had already let out a chuckle and responded.

“Yes, yes, yes! All the monsters are gone now, and there’s definitely no ghosts left. We can rest easy! Honestly, it’s quite stressful to be traveling with a procession of monsters.”

“Indeed! We must thank the immortal and the two great heroes for saving us. Otherwise, trapped deep within the ghost city, we surely would have met our demise.”

Ke Yundong, standing nearby, echoed this sentiment and gratitude.

“Ah, once is enough. Saying thank you ten times will just annoy people. A master like Mister Ji doesn’t care for such things. Come, come, let’s hurry to Luoping City.”

The Wei Family of Zhong Lake, established around Luoping City, owned a vast manor estate. Though not highly renowned in the martial world of the country of Zuyue currently, they were still a well-known name around Luoping City. The three Ke siblings and Zhou Xing were all from Luoping City.

Learning that they could travel alongside the immortal elder who saved their lives, as well as the two great heroes, the four were overjoyed.

Old Niu, walking briskly ahead with an arm around Ke Yundong and Zhou Xing’s shoulders, quietly whispered questions to the two of them.

“I have a question for you two. Luoping City is pretty big, right? Are there any famous places… hehehe… like that Red Beauty Brothel there?”

Seeing Old Niu, who looked like a simple farmer, ask such an obscene question with such a guileless expression, both Ke Yundong and Zhou Xing were somewhat bewildered.
