Chapter 325: The Fat and Cowardly General Giant Whale

Most of the torches that the villagers had initially carried with them were soaked and had gone out. Although the crisis seemed to have passed, the cold became even more biting.

“Hiss, it’s so cold! Master, can we leave now? ”

“Master, was that monster scared away? ”

“It’s freezing, I’m freezing to death! ”

The master was also rubbing his hands and wringing out his clothes, but thankfully, with spiritual energy protecting his body, he at least wasn’t trembling from the cold.

“Let’s go! The monster has been scared away, let’s get out of here! ”

“Everyone, move, move! ”

“Hurry, let’s go home, I’m freezing to death! ”

“Master, that monster just now was shouting for a powerful master to appear, is there another master nearby? ”

“That was a senior master from my sect. I had long sensed that the monster to the east was no ordinary one, so I specially invited a senior master of my grandmaster’s caliber to come down the mountain to assist. Now that the monster has fled, my elder must have chased after it, so everyone can rest assured!”

The crowd noisily rushed back. They had come in a grand, formidable manner, but now they returned in a scattered mess, most of the extinguished torches having been discarded.

“Watch your step, don’t trip, and don’t get too close to the shore! ”

“Everyone, keep an eye on your relatives and friends, see if anyone’s fallen behind!”

Some elders occasionally shouted amongst the group.

Zhang Fu and Liang Pingle, who were also running and shivering, heard those words and suddenly realized something.

“Oh no! Where’s Mr. Ji? ”

Zhang Fu’s loud shout made both the Liang and Zhang families stop dead in their tracks.

“Quick, search nearby, see if Mister Ji is there! ”

“Right, right, look quickly!”

“Mr. Ji-! Mister Ji, are you there?”

“Mister Ji! ”

The two families looked around, but all they saw were villagers hunched over, running back. A few torches lit the path ahead, but their light barely illuminated the crowd, and they couldn’t spot Ji Yuan for a long while.

Before long, almost everyone had passed them by, running ahead.

The four members of the Zhang and Liang families, both fathers and sons, exchanged glances. Only Zhang Fu still held a torch, barely illuminating a small area around them, but it swayed wildly in the sea breeze.

The direction they had come from was pitch black, making it seem as if some ferocious beast lurked beneath the sea.

Gritting his teeth, Zhang Fu’s father, a renowned captain in the village, spoke.

“Let’s go, we’ll go back and look again! ”

After a moment’s hesitation, the others followed Old Zhang back to where they had assembled for the torch formation.

Under the illumination of Zhang Fu’s sole remaining torch, the shoreline was littered with discarded damp torches, and the waves from the ocean crashed relentlessly against the rocks.

The distant sea was pitch black. Zhang Fu shone his torch down along the shoreline, but could see nothing but the white spray of the waves.

“If he fell down there…”

“Mr. Ji! Mr. Ji, are you there? Please come back to the village!”

“Mr. Ji…”

The group called out over the shore and the ocean several more times, but the only response was the mournful howl of the wind and the crashing of the waves. The flame of Zhang Fu’s torch flickered. It looked like it wouldn’t last much longer.

“Ugh, it’s so cold! We’re soaked to the bone, we can’t keep standing in the wind! ”

“Perhaps… perhaps Mr. Ji has already gone on ahead, and we just didn’t see him? ”

“That’s possible…”

“Yes, our torch won’t last much longer. Let’s hurry back. Maybe Mr. Ji is waiting for us!”

“That’s right, Mr. Ji is a capable man, he’s traveled all over the land and experienced so much. He will definitely be alright.”

Watching the torchlight of the large group ahead grow fainter in the distance, the cold and fearful group hesitated for a moment, then finally began to follow.

At this very moment, Ji Yuan was walking upon the waves, seemingly taking leisurely, unhurried steps, yet in reality, he was rapidly traveling over the surface of the sea, hardly any slower than he would travel on land.

If it had been Ji Yuan before he had achieved the Five Elements Returning to the Origin, while he could have done something similar, it wouldn’t have been so effortless; rather, it would have seemed arduous, far less convenient than flying directly or controlling the water to ride the waves.

But now that Ji Yuan was attuned to the Five Elements Qi, his movements were as light as spring, his speed unaffected by the ebb and flow of the waves. Not only was his spiritual energy consumption minimal, but he also felt ethereal, almost like a celestial being.

The General Giant Whale in the distance continued its frantic underwater escape, swimming further and further away from the shore, diving ever deeper.

Ji Yuan simply trailed behind, not entering the water himself, following until they were about a few dozen li offshore.

At this point, the General Giant Whale, who had been hundreds of zhang below the surface, slowly began to rise once again. As ocean spray slid off its back, its immense form was revealed once more.

The General Giant Whale gazed towards the distant shore, its tail swaying back and forth. Its fins couldn’t reach the rear part of its body, so it could only manipulate a current of water to brush against the underside of its lower spine.

There was a small spot there that was throbbing painfully, and hadn’t yet subsided. It was particularly intense, especially in the very moment of striking.

Just as an ordinary person would instinctively shake their hand if it were suddenly pricked by something, the General Giant Whale had also thrashed wildly. But it also possessed a strong sense of danger, and immediately fled towards the open sea.

Now, after fleeing for a while, and sensing no auras pursuing it, it cautiously rose to the surface to investigate.

The distant horizon was a stretch of pitch black, devoid of sound or light.

‘It seems they let me go?’

The General Giant Whale swam in circles a few times, hesitant about whether to leave this place and head for other waters.

‘To act on behalf of mortals… this must be one of those meddlesome immortal cultivators. It’s rare to encounter one, but they don’t seem very approachable.’

After a moment of hesitation, the General Giant Whale felt hunger gnawing at its belly, so it dove underwater and, opened its enormous jaws, and inhaled sharply.

On the surface, the waves didn’t seem to change much, but underwater, currents swirled, drawing countless fish of all sizes from afar, like a siphon draining water, feeding them directly into the General Giant Whale’s mouth.

The General Giant Whale’s mouth was like a bottomless pit, indiscriminately sucking in fish, shrimp, crabs, and everything in between. It took enough time for a dozen ordinary breaths before it finally closed its mouth.

“Grrr… Grrr… Grr…”1

A grinding sound emanated from the General Giant Whale’s belly.

Although Ji Yuan couldn’t see clearly underwater from his position on the surface, he could sense the whale’s imposing aura and the changes in the auras of the ocean as countless fish, shrimp, and crabs were drawn in.

No wonder the coastal fishermen couldn’t catch any fish; they weren’t scared away by evil spirits, they were simply being eaten by this General Giant Whale! It ate them wave after wave, so how could the fishermen catch anything?

After finishing its meal, the General Giant Whale wasn’t gluttonous; or rather, it was full. It surfaced again, still staring towards the shore it had fled from earlier.

Ji Yuan finally stopped waiting and revealed himself, speaking aloud.

“General Giant Whale, do you still have some lingering attachment to this coastline? ”

The voice right beside him startled the General Giant Whale cold.

“Oh, mother-!”2

It spun around around and saw a “tiny little creature” standing on the surface of the ocean, not too far away. It wasn’t riding the waves or controlling the water, but rather floating up and down alongside the waves, as if its body were as light as a goose feather.

“You… are you that senior master who just attacked me underhandedly? ”

“What harsh words. How can my strategy be called an underhanded attack? It was merely a minor punishment. With your ability to devour fish, can you not go anywhere to eat? Why must you linger by the shore? The coastal fishermen are practically starving to death. ”

Ji Yuan asked with a disdainful smile, then added.

“Also, you call yourself General Giant Whale. Which underwater palace’s general are you? And where are your troops? ”

Now that they were in deeper waters, the General Giant Whale was a little bolder.

“I am naturally General Giant Whale! This lord was appointed that title by my Lady Mother!3 As for my troops, hmph, none of them are worthy of my attention, so of course, I wouldn’t bring them along. My reasons for going to that part of the coast are my own business. What’s it to you? ”

Ji Yuan thought for a moment, then smiled and said to the General Giant Whale.

“You have a point. But what if I, Ji Yuan, still wish to know? ”

“Why? If I told you just because you wanted to know, what would happen to my reputation as the General Giant Whale? ”

Ji Yuan solemnly reached his hand skyward and grasped the Green Vine Sword that had materialized before him.

“This sword, wrapped in green vines, its sharpness concealed from the heavens, is an immortal sword. It wouldn’t even need to be fully drawn to slay a monster like you. So then, why would you tell me what I want to know? Naturally, it would be under duress!”

As he spoke, Ji Yuan gently drew out a third of the Green Vine Sword’s blade from the scabbard. A ribbon of pure white light seemed to sweep across the surrounding sea, dimming the starlight in the sky. A bone-chilling cold swept through the air. The General Giant Whale’s massive body froze completely.

This was the first time in its life that it had directly faced the might of an Immortal Sword. Merely seeing a portion of the Immortal Sword’s blade had its mind uncontrollably begin to conjure various illusions.

Illusions like a silver light cleaving it to pieces, like flying swords circling to strip its bones, like cold ice freezing it solid, and like sword light pressing down and crushing him…

Ji Yuan was merely trying to scare him, but the General Giant Whale couldn’t withstand it at all. It was truly a classic example of being ‘grand in body, cowardly of spirit,’ and it hurriedly cried out in a panic.

“Don’t kill me, don’t kill me! You’ll regret it if you kill me! I was waiting for Master Mo by the coast! I was waiting for Master Mo! ”

Ji Yuan sheathed the Green Vine Sword, his heart stirring slightly, but his face furrowed in a frown as he asked.

“Master Mo? Which Master Mo? What’s his background? Could he be an aquatic creature from the inland waters? ”

“Yes, yes, yes! Master Mo is a black flood dragon with profound cultivation. He passes by here every year. This time, I traveled a long, long way and waited for over a year, but he never arrived! I’m not an evil demon who likes to harm people! I truly am not! ”

‘Mo Rong! ’

Ji Yuan’s heart stirred, and that name naturally surfaced in his mind—it was indeed the black flood dragon, Mo Rong, who had died after fleeing back to Dazhen all those years ago.

T/n: All I could think of was “the whale has a built in garbage disposal!” XD

“Oh my god” but localized. Feel free to keep it as is or secretly headcanon the rest of the swear

母 (mǔ): mother/elderly female relative, 君 (jūn): monarch/lord. I briefly considered using “Regent Mother” but since I used Lord for 君 previously I’ll stick with Lady

Chapter 326: Everyone Has Their Moments of Cowardice

With this conjecture in mind, Ji Yuan didn’t bother beating around the bush; he directly questioned the giant whale.

“Is the ‘Master Mo’ you speak of named Mo Rong?”

Suddenly hearing Ji Yuan’s question, the giant whale, which had been chattering endlessly as it floated on the surface of the sea, instantly froze. One of its eyes cautiously watched Ji Yuan, its heart still pounding in trepidation. It wasn’t sure whether to answer yes or no: what if Master Mo had offended this person?

However, Ji Yuan didn’t need the giant whale to say it plainly; he could already tell he had guessed correctly from its reaction, and he directly continued.

“If you are referring to the black flood dragon who cultivated in the Wan Province of Dazhen, then you do not need to wait any longer. Mo Rong will not return.”

The scene Ji Yuan had witnessed years ago, of Mo Rong flowing away into the water after his death, flashed through his mind. Even now, thinking back on the moment that the remnants of his soul departed still filled him with a touch of melancholy. The Old Dragon’s fury was also still fresh in Ji Yuan’s memory.

Looking out at the distant sea, he wondered if that remnant soul had completed its journey “flowing away into the water” by now.1 Had it transformed into a wisp of true Qi, devoid of thought or desire, or had it found an opportunity to rebuild itself?

The ocean surface undulated, and the sea breeze continued to blow. The giant whale was clearly not all that stupid; after a short while, it vaguely considered some terrible possibilities. Looking at the Green Vine Sword in Ji Yuan’s hand, its heart trembled slightly.

“You, what did you do to Master Mo?”

Ji Yuan shook his head and looked at the giant whale again.

“I did nothing. Eight years ago, on Mo Rong’s way back from foraging in the Eastern Sea, he was ambushed by a large number of unidentified demons. He fought with all his might and self-destructed his Dragon Pearl just to escape back to Dazhen. Due to his severe injuries, his inner essence dissipated, and he fell from the sky, eventually perishing in Guangdong Lake soon after…”

Ji Yuan paused before continuing.

“Before his demise, I and the old gentleman Ying Hong stood beside Mo Rong’s corpse, watching his dragon soul depart to ‘flow away into the water.’”

The giant whale remained silent, seemingly still reeling from the information. After a long while, it murmured in disbelief.

“Impossible, impossible! Master Mo’s cultivation was so profound; he could crush me with a flick of his hand. How could he… how could he possibly be dead!”

‘Is Master Mo truly dead? Or is this man deceiving me? But Master Mo said he would go to the sea every year… Could something truly have happened to Master Mo? What will I do in that case? I can’t travel up the rivers or to the lakes, and I don’t know anyone there…’

Although it was a giant whale, Ji Yuan could sense its panic. As its massive body swayed, the surrounding ocean waves surged even more violently. Only when it finally calmed down a bit did Ji Yuan speak again.

“What business do you have with that black flood dragon? Or are you looking for the old gentleman Ying Hong? Is it you who seeks him, or the ‘Lady Mother’ you spoke of?”

Ji Yuan had instantly thought of many possibilities, and guessed that the ‘Lady Mother’ the giant whale had previously mentioned was the Old Dragon’s legitimate wife, the birth mother of the Dragon Son and Dragon Daughter.

The giant whale was clearly quite worried at this moment, even feeling utterly lost. Hearing Ji Yuan’s words, its worry became even more pronounced, and it mumbled to itself.

“I can only seek Master Mo, I only dared to seek Master Mo…”

As if suddenly and belatedly realizing something, it immediately looked at Ji Yuan.

“Immortal Master! Do you know the Dragon Lord?”

Earlier, Ji Yuan mentioned watching the black flood dragon’s soul flow away into the water with the Old Dragon, which clearly implied that he was friends with the Dragon Lord, not enemies. Otherwise, he wouldn’t have had the right to stand in such a place at such a time, as that moment was extremely important for those of the dragon lineage.

“I do.”

The giant whale gently stirred the seawater, subconsciously swimming a little closer, and asked in a low voice.

“Do you know him well?”

Ji Yuan looked at him and nodded.

“Very well.”

“Then, then… Can you call upon the Dragon Lord?”

The giant whale said, a little agitated and expectant.

Ji Yuan pondered for a moment. Given his friendship with the Old Dragon, and with a clear cause, it shouldn’t be difficult to ask him to personally come from Dazhen to meet this giant whale. So, he answered directly.

“I believe I can.”

“Then…”

The giant whale hesitated, dragging out a single word into a long sound before finally saying:

“How about– How about– How about asking the Immortal Master to… to help me inform Lady Ruoli? Or even Prince Feng would do!”

It was quite clear the giant whale was extremely afraid of the old dragon; it whimpered and sobbed for a long time, but ultimately only dared to request to call upon the Dragon Daughter and Dragon Son.

Ji Yuan didn’t provoke him further, nodding calmly.

“Alright, I will go call upon them for you. By the way, were you always seeking Mo Rong before? When was the last time you came?”

“I’ve never come seeking Master Mo before. The last time I saw him was probably… probably forty or fifty years ago. I can’t recall exactly.”

Ji Yuan understood. Recalling his conversation with Li Baotian, the City God of Lishun Prefecture back in Wan Province, he calculated that it was roughly around that time that the flood dragon Mo Rong had just taken residence in Guangdong Lake.

“Alright, wait here for now. I’ll make a trip to the Tongtian River in Dazhen. In the meantime, if you get hungry, try to find a suitable place to eat fish. Those fishermen were quite terrified of you.”

“Yes, yes, yes, this General— Er… I will certainly do as you say!”

At that point, the giant whale asked in confusion:

“Immortal Elder, aren’t you going to ask why I have come here?”

Ji Yuan smiled.

“It is most likely a family matter. If it’s convenient for me to know, then I will be informed once the person is found. I would also like to ask, do you trust me?”

The giant whale glanced at the Green Vine Sword, which had returned to Ji Yuan’s back. The immortal sword had still not yet concealed itself. The giant whale thought to himself, ‘how could I dare openly say that I don’t trust him?’

“I trust you! Immortal Master, you are clearly a profound master of the Tao. Hihgly accomplished immortal cultivators rarely lie. I trust you!”

“Good. In that case, I shall take my leave.”

Ji Yuan, unaware of the giant whale’s little schemes, took to the sky, riding on a cloud towards the southwest.

After about a quarter of an hour as Ji Yuan flew away, the giant whale, who had continuously and cautiously watching the distant night sky from the sea, quickly dove down with a ‘whoosh’ of displaced water.

‘First things first, better make a quick escape! ‘

…

Approximately a day and a half later, in the River God’s Underwater Palace in Dazhen’s Tongtian River.

The sky was already bright, but it was still the middle of winter, right before the New Year’s Eve, so there weren’t many boats navigating the Tongtian River at this time.

Ji Yuan descended from the sky on his cloud, directly plunging diagonally into the water, and after a small splash, he disappeared beneath the river surface.

He hadn’t actually visited the Tongtian River God’s underwater palace many times, but he remembered the route perfectly. He swam directly towards the correct location, bypassing the underwater barriers, and arrived at the front of the underwater palace.

Several underwater patrol yakshas spotted someone approaching on the water from afar and gathered around. When Ji Yuan drew closer, they recognized him and immediately bowed with cupped hands.

“We, the underwater Patrol Yakshas, greet Mr. Ji!”

Ji Yuan had already stopped as the Yakshas approached. He glanced at the faintly visible entrance to the underwater palace behind them, and returned the bow.

“No need for formalities. I have some business to attend to in the underwater palace. I need to see your Dragon Lord, as well as the River Goddess and His Highness Ying. Are they all within the palace? I must trouble you yakshas to inform them!”

The Yakshas looked at Ji Yuan. He emitted no trace of spiritual energy; the murky river waters naturally parted from him. Coupled with his demeanor and manner of speech, it was basically confirmed that he was who he said he was. Moreover, in Dazhen, who would dare impersonate Mr. Ji?

“Mister Ji, what are you saying? When you come to our Tongtian River underwater palace, there’s naturally no need to seek permission. Please, sir, follow me directly into the palace!”

“Very well, please lead the way.”

Although the Yakshas insisted, Ji Yuan could not simply set aside his courtesy.

After accepting his polite bow, two Yakshas led Ji Yuan towards the underwater palace, while the other two swiftly went ahead of them, leaving behind a trail of blurry bubbles as they entered the underwater palace to report.

Not long after, one of the Yakshas arrived at the underground dragon pool deep within the Tongtian River’s underwater palace. After passing the guards, it cautiously swam inside, approaching the massive, jet-black form looming in the darkness before it, and respectfully bowed.

“Reporting to the Dragon Lord, Mr. Ji has come to see you, the River Goddess, and His Highness Ying.”

From the darkness, it seemed as though two massive curtains lifted, revealing a glossy amber color behind them.

“Ji Yuan!”

He mumbled, then instantly transformed into a mist of light, solidifying into an old man in brocade robes. This was none other than Ying Hong’s true form. He then spoke to the Yaksha.

“Let’s go.”

“Yes, sir!”

Usually, when something was reported to the Dragon Lord, it would either be met with a “get lost ” or complete indifference. The Yaksha couldn’t help but marvel at Mr. Ji’s immense influence.

In the main hall of the underwater palace, Ying Ruoli had already arrived early, while Ying Feng happened to be absent.

The Old Dragon approached with unhurried steps. Rather than walking, his movements seemed to glide through the water. But before he could even reach the front hall from where he had entered at the back, he heard his daughter’s uncharacteristically loud cry of astonishment.

“Uncle Ji! Are you saying my mother might have sent someone to look for me?”

That shriek made the Old Dragon’s steps halt abruptly. His face contorted with an extremely complex and conflicted mix of expressions. He reached out and grabbed the Yaksha, who was still moving forward.

Startled, the yaksha tried to bow, but the Old Dragon quickly clamped a hand over his mouth.

The Old Dragon lightly tapped the Yaksha’s forehead with a finger, and a voice echoed in its mind.

“Go out and say you couldn’t summon me! ‘

The Yaksha’s stared wide-eyed at its Dragon Lord, expression utterly bewildered. Then, the voice sounded in its mind again.

“Just repeat what I told you. Also, Ji Yuan’s ears are very keen; don’t give anything away. No need to verbally receive my orders, just just go directly.”

The Yaksha understood. It nodded slightly, turned to glance towards the front hall, and when it looked back at the Dragon Lord, he was already gone.

The Dragon Lord’s current state was something the Yaksha had never witnessed since it had begun working in the underwater palace, but it didn’t dare think too much; it only knew the Dragon Lord’s command took precedence.

Chapter 327: The Origin

In the main hall, the Dragon Daughter, unlike her usual dignified and composed demeanor, was now visibly surprised and a little pale. Although most of her inner turmoil had been alleviated after Ji Yuan’s profound guidance at the birthday banquet those years ago, hearing news of her mother so suddenly still greatly agitated her.

“Uncle Ji, where did you see him? Why didn’t he come with you? “

The Dragon Daughter spoke several times faster than normal.

Ji Yuan answered frankly, without hiding anything.

“The other party is a giant whale, who calls himself General Giant Whale. He said it was a title bestowed upon him by the Lady Mother. He was waiting for Mo Rong somewhere along the coast of the Eastern Sea in the country of Zuyue, seemingly aware that Mo Rong went out to sea every year. I found him because his appetite was too immense; he ate all the fish along the coast, and the fishermen had to call in exorcists…”

Ji Yuan recounted everything in detail to the Dragon Daughter, finally concluding with:

“This whale is simply too massive to easily enter ordinary rivers, and he also seems to know only Mo Rong. As for whether your mother truly sent him, I am still uncertain. I need the River Goddess to accompany me to clarify the matter. “

“Mhm!”

The Dragon Daughter clasped her hands together, pacing a few steps from side to side, her long skirt swirling out like a blossoming flower.

Just then, a Yaksha hurried over from behind. After looking at the Dragon Daughter, he turned to Ji Yuan, cupping his hands in a bow.

“River Goddess, Mr. Ji, the Dragon Lord is in a deep sleep with no signs of waking. “

“What? Uncle Ji came personally to invite him! “

The Yaksha cautiously glanced at Ji Yuan, then clasped his hands and bowed to the Dragon Daughter.

“Begging your pardon, River Goddess, the Dragon Lord truly hasn’t woken. This humble one dared not disturb him further! “

The Dragon Daughter frowned. After glancing at the Yaksha, she turned to Ji Yuan.

“Uncle Ji, please wait here. I’ll go call my father! “

With that, the Dragon Daughter strode away, leaving only Ji Yuan and the Yaksha in the hall.

Ji Yuan smiled and nodded at the Yaksha, who hurriedly returned the smile.

“Mr. Ji, let me bring you some tea! “

“Alright, thank you. Please, bring some hot tea. “

“Yes, sir! “

The Yaksha seemed as though he had received some great pardon, and hurried away. He felt that Mr. Ji and the Dragon Lord exerted two entirely different kinds of “pressure”—indeed, it was pressure—both of which made him feel immense strain, especially in this current situation. He clearly felt that Master Ji had seen through his lie but simply chose not to expose it.

At this moment, the Dragon Daughter rushed towards the depths of the inner palace, quickly arriving at the cavern entrance to the Dragon Pool. Two Yakshas reached out to stop her.

“River Goddess, the Dragon Lord is in slumber. It is inadvisable to enter at this time.”

The Dragon Daughter said nothing, merely gave them a glance. The two Yakshas exchanged looks and withdrew their hands. She strode directly inside.

However, after only two steps, the Dragon Daughter found she could go no further. She reached out to touch the space in front of her, only to find that the water currents were incredibly dense. As she extended her hand into the water ahead, the resistance grew stronger the deeper she went, and she could advance no further than half a hand’s width, before finally being unable to advance.

‘A barrier technique! ’

“Father! Father, let me in! Uncle Ji has come to see you! Didn’t you complain last year that Uncle Ji had disappeared again? Weren’t you very curious after hearing about the True Fire of Samadhi? Uncle Ji himself is here! Father—”

The Dragon Daughter roared from outside. Meanwhile, the two Yakshas beside her found themselves in a difficult position, pleading earnestly.

“River Goddess, please keep your voice down. The Dragon Lord is resting! “

“Yes, River Goddess, if you keep shouting like this, our lives will be forfeit! “

Ying Ruoli frowned. After a moment’s hesitation, she roared again.

“Father, this time, it might be about Mother…”

She stopped mid-sentence. Ying Ruoli turned her head and found a hand resting on her shoulder. The Yakshas would never dare touch her. Turning fully, she saw that it was indeed Ji Yuan.

“Uncle Ji…”

Ji Yuan shook his head, transmitting his words in a low voice that only the Dragon Daughter could hear.

“You cannot wake someone who is pretending to be asleep—or rather, a dragon pretending to be asleep. It will have the same outcome if we go together to see the General Giant Whale. “

With that, Ji Yuan withdrew his hand and bowed towards the Dragon Pool.

“I will take my leave now, so as not to disturb Elder Ying’s rest! “

After speaking these words, Ji Yuan glanced at the Dragon Daughter, then turned and walked back the way he came. The latter hesitated for a moment, looked again at the entrance to the Dragon Pool, before finally giving up and turning to follow Ji Yuan.

Before long, the two figures left the underwater palace, swimming diagonally upwards towards the surface. They finally broke through the surface in an uninhabited section of the river, then stepped onto clouds and ascended into the sky.

However, about half an hour later, elsewhere on the Tongtian River, water rippled once more. A small stream parted to either side with a splash, and a blurry, invisible dragon-shaped aura rose into the sky from within the water.

Another day and a half later, Ji Yuan and the Dragon Daughter were now approaching the coastal region of the country of Zuyue. The Dragon Daughter had been deep in thought the entire journey, but now that they were arriving, she felt somewhat anxious.

“Uncle Ji, where is that giant whale?”

The Dragon Daughter meticulously observed the sea below, but apart from the surging waves, she could discern nothing else. She even manifested her true form and entered the ocean to search.

“I am not entirely sure. He might also be hiding; after all, he might not fully trust me…”

Ji Yuan smiled at the Dragon Daughter.

“But it’s no matter. Last time, I performed a little trick on him; I shot a wisp of sword Qi into his spine. I won’t have trouble finding him. “

“Omm…”

No sooner had Ji Yuan finished speaking did the Green Vine Sword behind him hum faintly, flashing in front of him.

“Go, drive him out.”

“Omm…”

With a soft hum, the Green Vine Sword’s blade flashed and vanished. Almost at the same instant, a ten-zhang long chasm split open in the sea below. The Green Vine Sword darted through it, the roiling seawater cut open by the immortal blade, creating a brief, waterless void in its wake.

“Let’s go, we’ll follow. “

With that, Ji Yuan and the Dragon Daughter once again took to the sky, following the Green Vine Sword from a distance.

“Sigh, my father really is something. Why didn’t he come! “

Ying Ruoli was still rather vexed at this moment. As Ji Yuan flew, he swept his gaze across the sky and various parts of the sea, then softly spoke, his eyes alight.

“He might not necessarily be absent. “

This was not because Ji Yuan truly sensed the Old Dragon’s presence, but rather a psychological deduction. If he were to put himself in the Old Dragon’s shoes, he would have secretly followed along to observe.

In the distance, a wisp of dragon mist carefully receded into the cloud bank.

‘Ji Yuan is far too perceptive! I came here with a split consciousness using the Mirage Transformation Technique, and he still sensed me!?’

Approximately a quarter of an hour later, within Ji Yuan’s and Ying Ruoli’s line of sight, a “bang!” suddenly echoed from the distant ocean surface, and large waves exploded into the sky.

“Don’t kill me… Immortal Master, don’t kill me—”

A giant whale actually leapt directly out of the water, soaring over a dozen zhang high into the air, then plummeted back down.

“Boom!”

With a deafening roar, the sea surface looked as though it had been struck by a colossal bomb.

A massive wave surged forth, crashing outwards in all directions, while a sudden downpour struck the surrounding area. The Green Vine Sword flashed amidst this “rain” and returned to floating directly behind Ji Yuan.

“I told you to wait patiently, why did you hide?”

Ji Yuan spoke calmly from the low-hanging clouds. He did not truly wish to hear the giant whale’s explanation, and simply gestured to the Dragon Daughter beside him.

“You should recognize him, yes? As for Elder Ying, he’s deeply asleep and can’t get up.”

Ji Yuan deliberately elongated the latter part of his sentence, his gaze casually sweeping the surroundings.

However, the giant whale clearly paid no attention to the second half of the matter. In fact, his attention was completely focused on Ying Ruoli.

“You… are you truly the River Goddess Ruoli? You look so much like the Lady Mother… “

The giant whale seemed to possess some special ability; despite this being their first meeting, he instantly recognized the Dragon Daughter. Lady. His deference and sincerity far surpassed that which he showed towards Ji Yuan.

Ying Ruoli had by now reverted to her usual goddess-like appearance, serene and divine, and she looked towards the giant whale.

“You are General Giant Whale, appointed such by my mother? Did my mother send you?”

The giant whale circled once on the water surface, answering truthfully.

“The Dragon Mother did not send me. How could she possibly mention such a thing? I secretly came to find Master Mo myself. I wouldn’t dare to disobey the Dragon Mother’s orders. I didn’t intend to alarm the Dragon Lord and the two Highnesses. I just didn’t expect Master Mo to…”

Ying Ruoli’s eyes dimmed slightly. So, it wasn’t her mother who sent him. Still, she pressed on.

“Then what brings you here? “

At this point, the giant whale did not conceal anything.

“About a hundred years ago, aquatic demons frequently appeared from beyond the Desolate Sea. Then, some twenty or thirty years ago, a malevolent flood dragon with profound cultivation arrived and pestered the Dragon Mother endlessly… The Dragon Mother, unable to bear the harassment, retreated beneath Dragon Rock Island for over a decade, never venturing out. I felt truly aggrieved, so I came to ask Master Mo for help in teaching that scoundrel a lesson… As for what happened next, this Immortal Master already knows! “

“What!? Such a thing exists? He must not want to live anymore! “

Ying Ruoli couldn’t hold back from exclaiming upon hearing this. As her brows furrowed solemnly and her draconic aura surged forth, her long hair scattering and billowing out, as if waves of invisible qi were radiating outwards from within her.

T/n: Curious family dynamics in the Ying family. Wonder what’s the drama between Ying Hong and his wife? Guess we’ll find out. Plus, JY casually tracking down this poor whale and telling him “I told you to wait patiently, why did you hide?” which let’s be real, is an objectively terrifying thing to hear from someone who can hunt you down across the ocean.

Chapter 328: Skillful Calligraphy

The Dragon Daughter’s anger flared, causing everything, from the sea breeze to the ocean waves, to grow even more tumultuous.

A wisp of mist began to rise near the horizon, seemingly turbulent. At this moment, a wisp of the Old Dragon’s spiritual consciousness appeared like a mirage within this mist, but his emotional state was quite similar to his daughter’s.

No matter what their relationship was like now, she was still the Old Dragon’s wife. Moreover, she had conceived Ying Ruoli and Ying Feng even before he transformed into a True Dragon. The bonds they once shared were etched into his very bones; how could he possibly forget?1

General Giant Whale sensed the impact of the surrounding waves, his massive body bobbing up and down in the seawater, and likewise grumbled in agreement, his voice filled with hatred.

“Her Highness is absolutely right! That vile demonic dragon is simply courting death! How dare he provoke the Lady Mother! If only my cultivation weren’t so shallow, I would have killed him long ago! “

“Hmph, even if Mo Rong isn’t here, I am! What kind of tactics does that demonic dragon use? How many demonic creatures does he have under his command? Never mind, don’t tell me. Take me there immediately! I’ll never let him go, even if it costs me my life! “

Ying Ruoli was indeed furious, but even in her rage, her words weren’t thoughtless. She knew she might not be able to persuade her father, but Uncle Ji was right beside her, and if he heard her say this, there was a high likelihood that he would help.

From the Dragon Daughter’s perspective, between her father and Uncle Ji, as long as one of them was willing to act, there was absolutely no chance of failure. Although she didn’t want to drag Uncle Ji into the karma of her family’s domestic affairs, she believed it would be fine to borrow some strength from him.

As expected, hearing Ying Ruoli declare she would fight to the death, Ji Yuan grew worried, and couldn’t just stand idly by. To his knowledge, although the Dragon Son Ying Feng was rather impulsive, Ying Ruoli had always been graceful and composed, rarely losing her composure like this. One could easily make mistakes in such circumstances, so he couldn’t just ignore it.

“River Goddess, please do not be rash. Since the other party is an evil flood dragon from beyond the Desolate Sea, and dares to harass your respected mother so persistently, they certainly possess some skill. “

Ying Ruoli took a deep breath to calm herself down, then said to Ji Yuan.

“Uncle Ji, I naturally know there must be some deeper cause, but as her daughter, how can my heart be at ease hearing such a tale? I have a presumptuous request, and I hope Uncle Ji might grant it. Of course, I will not let Uncle Ji bloody his hands! “

Hearing the Dragon Daughter actually call this immortal cultivator “Uncle,” the giant whale in the sea below secretly gasped in amazement.

Ji Yuan’s brows furrowed. After glancing at the giant whale on the surface, he looked directly at Ying Ruoli.

“What is the request? “

Ying Ruoli’s gaze swept towards the immortal sword on Ji Yuan’s back and said.

“I presumptuously dare to ask if I could borrow Uncle Ji’s Green Vine Immortal Sword. My father said that immortal artifacts possess spirits, and the Green Vine Sword is an extremely powerful immortal sword. Its blade has been concealed and its spirit nurtured for many years. If its full brilliance were to be revealed, even my father would be wary of it. I imagine that vile demon wouldn’t be able to withstand more than a few strikes. I implore you, Uncle Ji, please help me! “

The Old Dragon’s spiritual projection bobbed up and down in the mist above the water surface, his heart filled with anxiety.

‘My foolish daughter, the Green Vine Sword can be wielded freely in Ji Yuan’s hands, but can it be in yours? Whether the opponent can withstand it is one matter, but can you truly wield it? ‘

Ji Yuan felt the Green Vine Sword’s faint humming and trembling, evidently not keen on Ying Ruoli’s suggestion.

“Very well, how about this: I will accompany you on this journey. When we encounter that demonic dragon, if you feel he must be eliminated swiftly, I will then lend you the Green Vine Sword. With me by your side, the concealed blade will be more obedient, enabling you to strike with greater ease.”

Immortal artifacts had their own pride, and even the Green Vine Sword, which was deeply attached to Ji Yuan, was no exception. Ji Yuan understood this all too well.

Although there was the opportunity for compromise—for example, Ji Yuan could directly order the Green Vine Sword to guard the Dragon Daughter and strike down anyone she attacked—ultimately, he wouldn’t have felt entirely assured.

The Dragon Daughter did not stand on ceremony, and directly bowed in gratitude.

“Uncle Ji’s willingness to accompany me is simply ideal, I am endlessly grateful! Then, shall we depart immediately? “

Ji Yuan pondered for a moment and looked back towards the land.

“Wait a moment. I will make a trip to the seaside fishing village, then we can set off together. “

With that, Ji Yuan flew towards the land.

The Dragon Daughter thought for a moment, then spoke to the giant whale.

“You wait here for now, and do not wander off. “2

Then Ying Ruoli also flew towards the coast, soon vanishing from sight.

In Pianwan Village, a coastal village in the country of Zuyue, and indeed in all the surrounding villages and townships, a joyous atmosphere filled the air. This wasn’t just because the New Year was approaching, but also because the evil spirits that had plagued the coast for a long time had been driven away, promising a good catch for next year.

This wasn’t just based on what the Taoist priest had said, but also what the fishermen themselves had confirmed. Some people tried casting their nets and were already sporadically catching some fish. Although it wasn’t many, it was a tremendous improvement compared to how it was in the past.

However, the Zhang family and the Liang family in Qiangang Village remained somewhat somber these past few days.

This was because some time ago, on the night of the exorcism and torch formation, after everyone had retreated to their homes, everyone was accounted for except Mr. Ji.

It seemed unlikely that a stranger from an unfamiliar land would simply disappear on such a night. The possibility of him leaving without a word was slim.

The next day, they mobilized people to search the coast again, but naturally found nothing. Therefore, the Zhang and Liang families, along with the villagers from the surrounding area, all believed Ji Yuan had likely drowned at sea.

In the eyes of both families, this truly counted as dying in a foreign land. They felt a mix of guilt and fear, and even bought joss paper to the coast twice to burn it for him.

However, it was almost New Year after all, and coupled with the widespread optimism for next year’s catches, both families’ worries lessened, and they began to prepare for the New Year.

It was early morning when Ji Yuan returned to Pianwan Village. Zhang Fu and his wife were carrying wooden buckets and holding rags, wiping down their windows and doors, while his father was tidying up the fishing nets in the open space beside the house. Because every household had fishing nets and racks for drying fish, there was a large distance between each house.

Ji Yuan slowly approached, with Ying Ruoli following a few steps behind. The surrounding villagers paid no attention to their arrival. The only exception was when they reached the Zhang family’s doorstep. Zhang Fu had just finished wiping the main door and happened to turn around, then saw Ji Yuan standing right at his doorstep.

“Aaaah!”

“Thump! “

Zhang Fu thought he had seen a ghost and was so frightened that he fell over, dropping his bucket and splashing a puddle of muddy water.

Hearing the sound, Old Zhang also turned around to look, and his heart lurched upon seeing Ji Yuan.

“Old Mr. Zhang, Brother Zhang, do not be afraid.3 I am not a ghost. The morning sun has already risen, I am standing in the sunlight, and there is a shadow beneath my feet. How could I be a ghost! “

The Zhang father and son’s minds were unsettled. They cautiously looked at the ground, and indeed, there was a shadow. Moreover, it was broad daylight, thus it seemed unlikely Ji Yuan was a ghost.

This method of reasoning was actually a popular saying originating from folk tales. It wasn’t entirely accurate, at least not for some powerful ghosts, but it was a shortcut to make them believe.

“Mr. . . Mr. Ji, are you really alright? “

“I’m fine, I’m fine, what could possibly happen to me? It’s just that I was a bit impolite that night; when I saw the monster, I had already run away first, then went straight out of the village, running far away without looking back…”

Ji Yuan spoke with an embarrassed, apologetic expression.

“Later, when I heard that the Taoist priest had exorcised the demon, I felt a little guilty, so I came back to see you all. Otherwise, my conscience would never be at peace. Oh right, is the Liang family also alright? “

“No… no worries…”

Zhang Fu replied instinctively.

Hearing this, Ji Yuan finally smiled and bowed with cupped hands towards the two of them.

“I am relieved that you are well. Thank you for your hospitality these past few days, and thank you for coming back to look for me afterwards!”

“Oh, Sir, you are too kind…?”

“Yes we are also relieved to see that you are alright, sir! “

Ji Yuan’s explanation was indeed quite reasonable and also fit with common human nature. The father and son were truly relieved, offered Ji Yuan a few words of comfort.

“Hmm, I still feel a little guilty. Since I’m about to leave, I wanted to give you all a gift in return…”

Ji Yuan said, carefully choosing his words, but Zhang Fu and Old Zhang hastily declined.

“No need, no need. Sir, you’ve come from afar and are our guest, it’s only right for us to host you. You even helped us set up the torch formation…”

At this moment, the women and children of the Zhang family came out. Zhang Fu then explained a few things to make sure everyone in the family knew that Ji Yuan was still alive.

At this moment, Ji Yuan pretended to have just thought of something, and feigned a sudden realization.

“Oh right! You’re cleaning your homes in preparation for the New Year, aren’t you? “

Ji Yuan glanced at the wooden bucket and rag in Zhang Fu’s hand, and the areas around the Zhang family’s lintel.

“I am not very well-off, and I don’t have anything to offer. Since you are celebrating the New Year, you will certainly need some decorations. How about this: I’ll write a couple of ‘fortune’ characters4 for you and the Liang family. My calligraphy is quite decent, it’s not too bad! “

“This… is this appropriate?”

“That sounds good…”

The Zhang family was somewhat tempted. If Ji Yuan could write, it would save them the trouble of buying them at the market later.

“Perfect, perfect! I have my brush and ink with me, you just need to find two square sheets of red paper! “

“Oh, oh, we have that, we have that! Wife, quickly, find that big red paper we prepared to cut into sections to make red envelopes!”

Zhang Fu quickly instructed his wife, who promptly went into the house to look for it.

Afterward, the Zhang family once again cordially invited Ji Yuan inside for tea.

About fifteen minutes later, two large sheets of red paper were laid out on the square table inside the Zhang family’s house. Beside them was an inkstone with freshly ground ink. Ji Yuan held a thick brush in his hand.

The brush in Ji Yuan’s hand was a genuine wolf-hair brush. In these past few years on the island, as he cultivated, Ji Yuan personally crafted it in moments of enlightenment using a part of his spiritual essence. This was also the first time he was using it.

He dipped the brush in ink and wrote a bold “fortune” character on the red paper. At this moment, the Dragon Daughter, observing from nearby, seemed to be able to see faint red flowers faintly blossoming from the tip of Ji Yuan’s brush.

‘What skillful calligraphy!’5

Chapter 329: No Longer Laughing

Ji Yuan’s brush moved with divine inspiration. The character for “fortune,” written with rich ink, was slowly painted on both sheets of red paper.

Even though the Zhang family members were illiterate, watching Ji Yuan write still gave them a delightful sense of awe, both from his writing style, demeanor, and the characters themselves.

Once he had finished the “fortune” character, a subtle glow flashed and vanished. Ordinary eyes would at most only perceive the momentary glint as a trick of light, and wouldn’t look deeper into it.

However, after finishing the second character, Ji Yuan stopped.

Zhang Fu looked at the many sheets of square red papers still left on the table, scratched his head, and said:

“Mister Ji, there are still so many sheets of paper left. Would you mind writing a few more? We could share some with our neighbors and fellow villagers.”

But Ji Yuan had already put away his brush. He even picked up the inkstone, cradling it in his palm. Looking at the “fortune” characters on the table, whose ink was rapidly drying, he glanced over at Zhang Fu’s family and smiled.

“The calligraphy here is worth a thousand pieces of gold; it’s not something I can casually write more of. Put one of these two characters on the door of the Zhang family’s home, and send the other to the Liang family in Qiangang Village!”

Ji Yuan said as he began walking out, carrying the inkstone and brush. Outside the door, Ying Ruoli was already waiting.

Most of the Zhang family were still admiring the calligraphy. Only Zhang Fu and Old Zhang, seeing that Ji Yuan seemed about to leave, quickly stepped forward and tried to persuade him to stay.

“Mister Ji, please stay and have lunch with us. It would be even better if you could stay for a few days!”

“No, no. I actually have important matters to attend to, and someone is waiting for me, so I won’t stay long…”

As Ji Yuan left, Zhang Fu and Old Zhang saw him off. They looked outside but saw no one waiting, understanding that Ji Yuan was probably just being polite.

Old Zhang comforted him.

“Mister Ji, please don’t worry about what happened a few days ago. Your well-being sets our minds at ease for a peaceful New Year. Besides, you’ve left us with your calligraphy. Your hometown is very far, and with the New Year approaching, you surely won’t make it back in time. There’s really no need to rush off like this…”

Ji Yuan felt quite touched upon hearing these words, especially considering the rather unfriendly local customs in many parts of the country of Zuyue. He then turned and bowed with cupped hands to Old Zhang again.

“That’s not the reason. I’m actually quite thick-skinned, but I truly have matters that prevent me from staying. Oh, and about these two ‘fortune’ characters, if anyone wishes to buy them…”

Ji Yuan’s smile faded, his expression becoming slightly serious.

“No matter who offers money, or how much, do not sell them! Give the same instructions to the Liang family, understand?”

Seeing Ji Yuan’s serious expression, the Zhang father and son exchanged a glance and instinctively nodded in unison.

“We’ll remember.”

“Good. Then I shall depart. You two need not see me off.”

With that, Ji Yuan turned and strode away. After taking a dozen steps, the inkstone and brush in his hand had vanished.

In just a moment, Ji Yuan’s figure had vanished from Zhang Fu and Old Zhang’s sight. They had originally intended to see him off further, but after walking around a corner of the house, they could no longer see any trace of Ji Yuan.

“Mr. Ji walks so fast?”

Old Zhang looked at his son with a hint of surprise. The latter also seemed a bit puzzled.

“Never mind. Isn’t Chunlan going back to her family home? It’s perfect, let her take this ‘fortune’ character calligraphy with her. Also, don’t forget what Mr. Ji just said about not selling it.”

“I know, I know. I’ll go with Chunlan to her father’s house.”

The father and son chatted as they returned indoors. They still needed to tidy up their home thoroughly to welcome the New Year with a fresh start.

…

In the sky, flying towards the distant ocean, Ying Ruoli looked back at the rapidly receding fishing village and asked Ji Yuan,

“Uncle Ji, was that an Immortal Edict just now?”

“What ‘Immortal Edict’? An Edict is an Edict; there’s no need to forcibly add the title ‘Immortal.’ Your father could achieve the same effect.”

Ji Yuan replied with a slight smile, then flew with Ying Ruoli towards the location of the Giant Whale, quickly returning to that same spot in the sea.

As they arrived, the Giant Whale slowly emerged from beneath the sea surface once more.

“River Goddess Ying Ruoli, Immortal Elder Ji, if you don’t mind, please stand upon this humble general’s back. My underwater speed isn’t all that slow, and I know many underwater shortcuts, allowing us to bypass numerous strange currents in the Heavenly Domain Seas, and avoid getting lost!”

Ying Ruoli nodded slightly to Ji Yuan, inviting him on with a gesture of her hand.

“Uncle Ji, please!”

“Alright, let’s take a ride on the giant-whale-boat.”1

The two descended from the sky and stood together on the whale’s back. The giant whale flicked its tail, pushing aside a large volume of seawater, and began swimming towards the horizon, its speed increasing.

However, at this moment, when he heard about the strange currents in the Heavenly Domain Sea, Ji Yuan suddenly realized a problem.

“By the way, River Goddess, do you know where Dragon Rock Island, where your mother resides, is located?”

The Dragon Daughter shook her head.

“I don’t know. Mother used to live in the Eastern Sea, then she left for somewhere else and never returned. Uncle Ji, we’re already very far from the Tongtian River now, so please don’t call me River Goddess anymore. Just ‘Ruoli’ is fine.”

Ji Yuan forced a smile. Now he was concerned about a different matter.2 He looked down at the Giant Whale swiftly swimming below them.

“May I ask, General Giant Whale, where is Dragon Rock Island, where your Lady Mother resides?”

“The Lady Mother never allows it to be spoken of, especially by those from this region… But it should be fine for Immortal Elder Ji and River Goddess Ying Ruoli to know, since we’re headed there anyway. Actually, Dragon Rock Island isn’t within the Eastern Sea. One must pass through a vast expanse of ocean, detour around a portion of the Desolate Sea, and reach the border between the Eastern Sea and the Northern Sea.”

Bypass a portion of the Desolate Sea? The border between the Eastern Sea and the Northern Sea?

Ji Yuan tried to imagine it. This probably meant going east to the edge of the Eastern Sea, then going up close to the Northern Sea. Wasn’t that the furthest northeastern corner within the Desolate Sea?

“That far?”

Ji Yuan couldn’t help but ask. He could hardly imagine how far that distance would be. All the continents in the world were surrounded by oceans, and the area covered by the Eastern Sea undoubtedly many times larger than that of the Eastern Cloud Continent.

“Your mother sure ran quite far…”

Ji Yuan made a rare remark. Even Ying Ruoli could only offer an awkward smile, unsure how to respond.

“It is a bit far, but rest assured, Immortal Elder Ji. This humble General Giant Whale may not have that many skills, but I definitely know how to find my way across the oceans! Those realm-crossing ferries, flying ships, and floating islands—in terms of speed, they cannot compare to me!”

General Giant Whale spoke as he swam, looking quite smug, while Ji Yuan’s heart grew heavy.

“River Goddess Ying Ruoli, you are an orthodox river goddess. Is it alright for you to be away from the Tongtian River for so long?”

Ying Ruoli smiled lightly.

“Uncle Ji, there’s no need to worry. All the aquatic creatures in my Tongtian River serve my underwater palace. Whatever problem might arise, someone will help me resolve it. Besides, I don’t truly care for the divine path, so it doesn’t matter how long I’m outside my jurisdiction.”

“Oh… that’s good…”

Ji Yuan truly couldn’t manage a smile anymore.

In the patch of ocean behind the departing giant whale, a mirage-like dragon aura hidden within a patch of mist sensed the whale’s departure. What the old dragon’s divided consciousness perceived naturally represented what his True Dragon body perceived.

‘With Ji Yuan tagging along, there shouldn’t be any major problems, right? ‘

…

In Pianwan Village, Zhang Fu, his wife Liang Chunlan, and their young son carried some gifts and traveled to Qiangang Village, seven or eight li away. They arrived at the Liang family’s home before lunch.

The visiting family put down the basket of items they had brought, while the host family poured water in welcome. Naturally, they shared a warm conversation, which inevitably, led to discussions about Ji Yuan.

“Brother-in-law, you mean Mr. Ji isn’t dead?”

Liang Pingle’s face lit up with surprise, and his father also looked astonished.

“That’s right, Mr. Ji is fine. He came to our house earlier this morning to let us know he was safe and sound, and even wrote a ‘fortune’ character for us!”

Zhang Fu recounted how he had met Ji Yuan that morning with a smile, narrating how the latter had written the characters for them and explaining everything in detail.

“By the way, look, this is the ‘fortune’ character Mister Ji wrote. Before I came, I specifically walked around the entire village, and none of the characters pasted on any of the houses were as beautiful as Mr. Ji’s! Not even half as beautiful!”

As Zhang Fu spoke, he deliberately stood up, left the table, walked to the basket he had set aside, rummaged through it, pulling out rolled-up piece of red paper.

“Look!”

The red paper unrolled, revealing a boldly-written “fortune” character in thick ink. Just looking at it made the Liang family feel quite happy.

“What beautiful calligraphy, truly beautiful calligraphy!”

“Indeed, it’s really beautiful!”

The Liang family members likewise couldn’t read many characters, yet they commented on it as if they were old scholars.

“See? I wasn’t lying to you. Mr. Ji said this calligraphy is worth a thousand gold pieces. Hmm, it probably means it’s priceless. He told us to paste it on our doors, and no matter who sees it and wants to buy it, no matter how much money they offer, do not sell it!”

“We won’t sell it, we won’t sell it!”

“Exactly! This is a New Year’s blessing from Mr. Ji, why would we sell it?”

“Yes, this calligraphy is truly wonderful!”

Hearing that Ji Yuan was alive also brought a slight sense of relief to the Liang father and son. At least they could have a more relaxed New Year, without carrying any lingering resentments. And seeing this “fortune” character made their mood exceptionally cheerful.

Liang Pingle eagerly grabbed the “fortuen” character and ran to the door, measuring it up, down, left, and right, trying to see how it should be pasted correctly.

Liang Pingle’s father laughed from inside the house and scolded him.

“You silly child, are you daft? Can you paste it up just by holding it to the door? Wait for your mother to make some paste from rice porridge first, then you can stick it!”

Liang Pingle chuckled, thinking that his father was correct, he must have been too eager. He could only blame it on the fact that the calligraphy was too beautiful.

Just as he was thinking this, he loosened his grip, and the “fortune” character suddenly stuck itself to the door.

Liang Pingle thought it had been blown there by the wind, and scratched his head, attempting to peel it off. However, he found that his fingernails couldn’t even get under the edge of the paper, and he instantly froze in surprise.

“It’s stuck on?”
